```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_merge_log_patient
    ON m11_refractive_merge_log (target_patient_id, apply_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
```

**约束：**

```sql
-- 记录号格式：BH + 8 位日期 + 4 位流水
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_no_format
    CHECK (merge_no ~ '^BH[0-9]{12}$');

-- 操作类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_type
    CHECK (merge_type IN ('MERGE', 'SPLIT', 'CORRECT', 'REDIRECT'));

-- 合并类操作必须有源档案
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_source
    CHECK (merge_type NOT IN ('MERGE', 'REDIRECT')
           OR (source_record_id IS NOT NULL AND source_record_no IS NOT NULL));

-- 源档案与目标档案不能是同一份
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_not_self
    CHECK (source_record_id IS NULL OR target_record_id <> source_record_id);

-- 纠错类操作必须有表名、目标行与原因
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_correct
    CHECK (merge_type <> 'CORRECT'
           OR (target_table IS NOT NULL AND target_row_id IS NOT NULL
               AND target_field IS NOT NULL
               AND old_value IS NOT NULL AND new_value IS NOT NULL
               AND correct_reason IS NOT NULL
               AND char_length(btrim(correct_reason)) >= 10));

-- 纠错前后值必须不同
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_correct_diff
    CHECK (merge_type <> 'CORRECT' OR old_value <> new_value);

-- 影响范围枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_impact
    CHECK (impact_scope IS NULL
           OR impact_scope IN ('NONE', 'PLAN_AFFECTED', 'POSTOP_STAT_AFFECTED'));

-- 执行状态枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_exec
    CHECK (exec_status IN ('PENDING', 'EXECUTING', 'DONE', 'FAILED', 'REJECTED'));

-- 审核结论枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_audit
    CHECK (audit_result IS NULL OR audit_result IN ('APPROVED', 'REJECTED'));

-- 不能自审
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_self_audit
    CHECK (audit_by IS NULL OR audit_by <> apply_by);

-- 已审核必须有审核人与时间
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_audited
    CHECK (audit_result IS NULL OR (audit_by IS NOT NULL AND audit_at IS NOT NULL));

-- 驳回必须写审核意见
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_rejected
    CHECK (exec_status <> 'REJECTED'
           OR (audit_result = 'REJECTED' AND audit_note IS NOT NULL
               AND char_length(btrim(audit_note)) >= 5));

-- 已完成必须有执行时间
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_done
    CHECK (exec_status <> 'DONE' OR exec_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 执行失败必须有错误信息
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_failed
    CHECK (exec_status <> 'FAILED'
           OR (exec_error IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(exec_error)) >= 5));

-- 回滚必须有时间与原因
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_rollback
    CHECK (rollback_flag = FALSE
           OR (rollback_at IS NOT NULL AND rollback_reason IS NOT NULL
               AND char_length(btrim(rollback_reason)) >= 5));

-- 申请理由 ≥ 10 字
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_apply_reason
    CHECK (char_length(btrim(apply_reason)) >= 10);

-- 迁移统计非负
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_counts
    CHECK (moved_baseline_count >= 0 AND moved_baseline_eye_count >= 0
       AND moved_surgery_history_count >= 0 AND moved_medical_history_count >= 0
       AND moved_timeline_event_count >= 0 AND conflict_count >= 0);

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_refractive_merge_log IS '档案合并与纠错记录表：记录合并/拆分/字段纠错全过程，含迁移量级、字段级前后值、申请与审核双人留痕';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_merge_log.merge_type IS '操作类型：MERGE 合并 / SPLIT 拆分 / CORRECT 字段纠错 / REDIRECT 患者号重定向';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_merge_log.old_value IS '纠错前的字段值，必须与原行实际值一致（由服务端读取而非客户端传入）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_merge_log.impact_scope IS '影响范围：PLAN_AFFECTED 表示影响手术方案，POSTOP_STAT_AFFECTED 表示影响术后统计，需要在质控报表中标注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_merge_log.audit_by IS '审核人，与申请人不得为同一人（双人复核）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_merge_log.rollback_flag IS '是否已回滚；合并与纠错均支持在 24 小时内回滚，超期需走数据修复工单';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么合并要留迁移行数：** 合并是破坏性操作（源档案被置 `MERGED`）。留 `moved_*_count` 六项统计，使回滚与事后对账有量化依据；若只记录「合并成功」，一旦发现合错（同名患者），无法判断原始数据量是否完整还原。
2. **为什么要求双人复核：** 档案合并直接影响手术方案的历史归属与质控统计口径；单人操作容易在患者同名场景下误并，双人复核是眼科质控的通行要求。
3. **为什么 `old_value` 必须由服务端读取：** 若接受客户端传入的「原值」，恶意或错误请求可伪造历史值，导致审计链失真；服务端在同一事务内先 `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` 读取原值再写入，保证 `old_value` 与真实历史一致。
4. **为什么 `impact_scope` 分三档：** 修改术前基线屈光度会改变切削计算与术后可预测性统计（`POSTOP_STAT_AFFECTED`），必须在质控报表中标注，否则 M11-07 的质量指标会被静默污染。
5. **为什么支持 24 小时内回滚：** 实际运营中「刚合并完发现并错」是最常见的错误形态；在时间窗内回滚可以完全恢复（源档案 `MERGED → ACTIVE`，从表 `record_id` 回改），超期后因下游方案与统计已消费新归属，只能走数据修复工单并人工核对。

**分区策略：** 无（800/年，量级极小）。

---

### 4.9 表：`m11_refractive_change_log`（档案变更审计日志表）

**用途：** 记录屈光档案上**每一次写操作**的字段级变更（新增、修改、状态流转、删除），append-only，不修改不删除。它服务于：电子病历修改留痕（法规要求）、医疗责任界定、以及「术前基线是否被事后修改过」的质控核查。

**为什么与 4.8 合并纠错表并存：** 4.8 只记录**治理类**操作（合并/纠错/拆分），是低频、需审批的高风险动作；本表记录**全部**常规写操作（录入、修改、确认、状态流转），是高频、量大的审计流。两者记录粒度与用途不同，合并会导致 4.8 表被高频日志淹没而失去「审批流水」的清晰语义。

**写入方：** M11-03 全部写操作（由 Service 层统一埋点）。**只读方：** P08 变更历史、M02-06 审计、M22 质控追溯。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键（分区表主键需包含分区键 `created_at`） |
| log_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 日志流水号 `CL` + `YYYYMMDD` + 6 位（如 `CL20261031000087`） |
| record_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 屈光档案 ID（档案级操作必填） |
| record_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 档案号冗余 |
| patient_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 患者 ID（M03-01，只读引用） |
| org_id | BIGINT | N | 1 | 院区 ID |
| **操作对象组** | | | | |
| object_type | VARCHAR(32) | N | 'RECORD' | 操作对象类型 `RECORD`（档案主表）/ `BASELINE`（基线）/ `BASELINE_EYE`（基线眼别）/ `SURGERY_HISTORY`（手术史）/ `MEDICAL_HISTORY`（既往史）/ `TIMELINE`（时间轴）/ `MERGE`（合并纠错）/ `EXPORT`（导出） |
| object_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 对象主键 ID |
| object_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 对象业务单号（如基线编号、记录号） |
| **动作组** | | | | |
| action | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 操作动作 `CREATE`（新建）/ `UPDATE`（修改）/ `DELETE`（逻辑删除）/ `CONFIRM`（确认）/ `SUBMIT`（提交）/ `AUDIT`（审核）/ `REJECT`（驳回）/ `MERGE`（合并）/ `CORRECT`（纠错）/ `CLOSE`（结档）/ `REOPEN`（重新激活）/ `IMPORT`（导入）/ `EXPORT`（导出）/ `PRINT`（打印）/ `SYNC`（同步） |
| action_label | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 动作中文名快照 |
| from_status | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 变更前状态 |
| to_status | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 变更后状态 |
| **变更明细组** | | | | |
| changed_fields | JSONB | Y | NULL | 字段级变更明细 `{"sphere":{"old":"-4.25","new":"-4.50"}}` |
| change_summary | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 变更摘要（人类可读，如「球镜 -4.25D → -4.50D，柱镜 -1.25D → -1.75D」） |
| field_count | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 本次变更字段数 |
| sensitive_field_hit | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否涉及敏感字段（屈光度/角膜厚度/手术史等临床关键字段） |
| reason | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 操作原因（修改临床关键字段、删除、合并时必须填） |
| **操作者与环境组** | | | | |
| operator_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 操作人 ID（M01-02，只读引用） |
| operator_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 操作人姓名快照 |
| operator_role | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 操作人角色 `DOCTOR`（屈光医师）/ `NURSE`（护士）/ `TECHNICIAN`（技师）/ `CONSULTANT`（咨询师）/ `ADMIN`（管理员）/ `SYSTEM`（系统） |
| client_type | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 客户端类型 `WEB` / `PAD` / `MOBILE` / `API` / `JOB` |
| ip_address | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 客户端 IP（VARCHAR 兼容 IPv6 与代理链） |
| trace_id | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 链路追踪 ID（关联运行日志） |
| request_id | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 请求 ID |
| **分区与标准字段** | | | | |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 操作时间（**分区键**） |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除（审计记录，正常永不为 TRUE） |

**索引：**

```sql
-- 日志号唯一（含分区键）
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m11_refractive_change_log_no
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (log_no, created_at);

-- 按档案查变更历史（P08 主力索引）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_change_log_record
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (record_id, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按对象查（某条基线的全部变更）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_change_log_object
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (object_type, object_id, created_at DESC)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE AND object_id IS NOT NULL;

-- 敏感字段变更专项审计（屈光度/厚度等临床关键字段）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_change_log_sensitive
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (record_id, created_at DESC)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE AND sensitive_field_hit = TRUE;

-- 按操作人审计
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_change_log_operator
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (operator_id, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按动作统计
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_change_log_action
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (action, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按患者查
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_change_log_patient
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (patient_id, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按院区 + 类型（跨院区审计）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_change_log_org
    ON m11_refractive_change_log (org_id, object_type, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
```

**约束：**

```sql
-- 日志号格式：CL + 8 位日期 + 6 位流水
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_no_format
    CHECK (log_no ~ '^CL[0-9]{14}$');

-- 对象类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_object
    CHECK (object_type IN ('RECORD', 'BASELINE', 'BASELINE_EYE', 'SURGERY_HISTORY',
                           'MEDICAL_HISTORY', 'TIMELINE', 'MERGE', 'EXPORT'));

-- 动作枚举（15 种）
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_action
    CHECK (action IN ('CREATE', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'CONFIRM', 'SUBMIT', 'AUDIT',
                      'REJECT', 'MERGE', 'CORRECT', 'CLOSE', 'REOPEN', 'IMPORT',
                      'EXPORT', 'PRINT', 'SYNC'));

-- 操作人角色枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_role
    CHECK (operator_role IS NULL
           OR operator_role IN ('DOCTOR', 'NURSE', 'TECHNICIAN', 'CONSULTANT',
                                'ADMIN', 'SYSTEM'));

-- 客户端类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_client
    CHECK (client_type IS NULL
           OR client_type IN ('WEB', 'PAD', 'MOBILE', 'API', 'JOB'));

-- 修改类动作必须有字段级明细
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_update
    CHECK (action <> 'UPDATE'
           OR (changed_fields IS NOT NULL AND change_summary IS NOT NULL
               AND field_count IS NOT NULL AND field_count > 0));

-- 状态流转类动作必须有前后状态
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_transition
    CHECK (action NOT IN ('CONFIRM', 'SUBMIT', 'AUDIT', 'REJECT', 'CLOSE', 'REOPEN')
           OR (from_status IS NOT NULL AND to_status IS NOT NULL
               AND from_status <> to_status));

-- 修改敏感字段必须写原因（≥ 5 字）
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_sensitive_reason
    CHECK (sensitive_field_hit = FALSE
           OR (reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(reason)) >= 5));

-- 删除与合并必须写原因
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_reason
    CHECK (action NOT IN ('DELETE', 'MERGE', 'CORRECT')
           OR (reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(reason)) >= 5));

-- 非系统操作必须有操作人
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_change_log_operator
    CHECK (operator_role = 'SYSTEM'
           OR (operator_id IS NOT NULL AND operator_name IS NOT NULL));

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_refractive_change_log IS '档案变更审计日志表：记录全部写操作的字段级变更，append-only，按 created_at 月度分区保留 36 个月';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_change_log.changed_fields IS '字段级变更明细 JSONB，结构 {"字段名":{"old":旧值,"new":新值}}';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_change_log.sensitive_field_hit IS '是否涉及临床关键字段（屈光度/角膜厚度/轴位/手术史），为 TRUE 时必须填写 reason';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_change_log.created_at IS '分区键：按 created_at 月度 RANGE 分区，含 DEFAULT 分区兜底';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_change_log.trace_id IS '链路追踪 ID，与运行日志的 trace_id 一致，便于联合排查';
```

**分区策略：**

append-only 大表（约 300,000 行/年），按 `created_at` **月度 RANGE 分区**，保留 **36 个月**（电子病历审计留痕的通行保留期），并带 **DEFAULT 分区兜底**：

```sql
CREATE TABLE m11_refractive_change_log (
    id                 BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
    log_no             VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    record_id          BIGINT,
    record_no          VARCHAR(32),
    patient_id         BIGINT,
    org_id             BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    object_type        VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RECORD',
    object_id          BIGINT,
    object_no          VARCHAR(32),
    action             VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    action_label       VARCHAR(64),
    from_status        VARCHAR(24),
    to_status          VARCHAR(24),
    changed_fields     JSONB,
    change_summary     VARCHAR(512),
    field_count        SMALLINT,
    sensitive_field_hit BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    reason             VARCHAR(512),
    operator_id        BIGINT,
    operator_name      VARCHAR(64),
    operator_role      VARCHAR(32),
    client_type        VARCHAR(16),
    ip_address         VARCHAR(64),
    trace_id           VARCHAR(64),
    request_id         VARCHAR(64),
    created_at         TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    is_deleted         BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);

CREATE TABLE m11_refractive_change_log_y2026m10
    PARTITION OF m11_refractive_change_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01') TO ('2026-11-01');
CREATE TABLE m11_refractive_change_log_y2026m11
    PARTITION OF m11_refractive_change_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01');
CREATE TABLE m11_refractive_change_log_y2026m12
    PARTITION OF m11_refractive_change_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-12-01') TO ('2027-01-01');

-- DEFAULT 分区兜底（共享规范 2.6）
CREATE TABLE m11_refractive_change_log_default
    PARTITION OF m11_refractive_change_log DEFAULT;

-- 保留 36 个月：超期分区导出 JSONL 后 DETACH + DROP
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION fn_m11_changelog_drop_expired(p_keep_months INT DEFAULT 36)
RETURNS INT
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_rec RECORD;
    v_cnt INT := 0;
    v_cut DATE := (date_trunc('month', CURRENT_DATE)
                   - make_interval(months => p_keep_months))::DATE;
BEGIN
    FOR v_rec IN
        SELECT c.relname AS part_name,
               substring(c.relname FROM 'y(\d{4})m(\d{2})') AS ym
        FROM pg_class c
        JOIN pg_inherits i ON i.inhrelid = c.oid
        JOIN pg_class p ON p.oid = i.inhparent
        WHERE p.relname = 'm11_refractive_change_log'
          AND c.relname ~ '^m11_refractive_change_log_y\d{4}m\d{2}$'
    LOOP
        IF to_date(v_rec.ym, 'YYYYMM') < v_cut THEN
            EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_change_log DETACH PARTITION %I',
                           v_rec.part_name);
            EXECUTE format('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS %I', v_rec.part_name);
            v_cnt := v_cnt + 1;
        END IF;
    END LOOP;
    RETURN v_cnt;
END;
$$;
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么日志要物化到本表而不是只写运行日志：** 运行日志（ELK）保留期通常 30~90 天，而屈光档案的医疗责任追溯期以年计（涉及术后并发症与再手术）；且运行日志按 trace 检索，无法高效回答「这条基线被谁改过几次」这类档案维度问题。
2. **为什么 `sensitive_field_hit` 单独标记：** 屈光度、角膜厚度、轴位、手术史是手术方案与质控指标的直接输入，修改它们等同于修改医疗决策依据；单列该标记可让质控快速拉出「临床关键字段被修改」的清单，而不必解析全部 `changed_fields`。
3. **为什么按 `created_at` 而非业务日期分区：** 审计日志的价值在于「记录发生的时刻」，业务日期可被伪造或补录；按入库时间分区保证分区边界不可被业务数据操纵。
4. **为什么必须带 DEFAULT 分区：** 若分区预建失败或时钟异常导致写入未来时间，无 DEFAULT 分区会直接插入失败并阻塞业务写操作；DEFAULT 兜底保证「日志写入永不失败」，事后由告警任务补建分区并把数据迁移过去。
5. **为什么 `field_count` 单独存：** 审计统计常需回答「单次操作修改了多少字段」（大批量修改是异常信号）；从 JSONB 现算 `jsonb_object_keys` 统计会退化为全表扫描，物化后可直接聚合。

**分区策略：** 月度 RANGE 分区（`created_at`）+ DEFAULT 兜底，保留 36 个月。

---

### 4.10 表：`m11_refractive_export_task`（档案打印与导出任务表）

**用途：** 管理屈光档案的**异步打印与导出任务**（单档 PDF 病程摘要、批量导出 Excel、质控口径导出），记录任务参数、进度、文件地址、有效期与操作人，支持任务重试与到期清理。

**为什么必须异步并单独建表：**
1. 单档完整病程 PDF 需汇总 9 张表 + 跨模块指针，渲染耗时 2~8 秒，同步接口会超时；
2. 批量导出（如全院高度近视档案导出）可达数万行，必须后台生成并分片；
3. 导出文件含患者隐私（姓名、屈光度、手术史），必须记录「谁导出了什么」并设过期自动清理，是合规要求。

**写入方：** M11-03（P07 打印/导出操作）。**只读方：** P07 页面轮询任务状态、M02-06 审计（导出留痕）。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **标识组** | | | | |
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| task_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 任务号 `DC` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位流水（如 `DC202610310003`） |
| org_id | BIGINT | N | 1 | 院区 ID |
| **任务类型与范围组** | | | | |
| task_type | VARCHAR(24) | N | 'RECORD_PDF' | 任务类型 `RECORD_PDF`（单档病程摘要 PDF）/ `RECORD_PRINT`（档案打印）/ `BATCH_EXPORT`（批量导出 Excel）/ `SURGERY_HISTORY_EXPORT`（手术史导出）/ `BASELINE_EXPORT`（基线数据导出）/ `QUALITY_EXPORT`（质控口径导出） |
| record_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 档案 ID（单档任务必填） |
| record_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 档案号 |
| patient_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 患者 ID（单档任务必填） |
| scope_type | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'SINGLE' | 范围类型 `SINGLE`（单档）/ `MULTI`（多档，按筛选条件）/ `ALL`（全量，需审批） |
| filter_json | JSONB | Y | NULL | 筛选条件（`MULTI` / `ALL` 时保存，如 `{"is_high_myopia":true,"date_from":"2026-01-01"}`） |
| record_count | INT | Y | NULL | 涉及档案数量（生成后回填） |
| template_code | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 打印模板编码（印刷模板版本，如 `TPL_RECORD_V2`） |
| export_format | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'PDF' | 文件格式 `PDF` / `XLSX` / `CSV` / `JSON` |
| include_images | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否包含影像（地形图/角膜厚度图，会显著增大文件） |
| **状态与进度组** | | | | |
| task_status | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'PENDING' | 任务状态 `PENDING`（排队）/ `RUNNING`（生成中）/ `DONE`（已完成）/ `FAILED`（失败）/ `EXPIRED`（文件已过期）/ `CANCELLED`（已取消） |
| progress_pct | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 进度百分比（0~100） |
| started_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 开始生成时间 |
| finished_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 完成时间 |
| error_message | VARCHAR(1024) | Y | NULL | 失败原因 |
| retry_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 重试次数 |
| **文件组** | | | | |
| file_name | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 文件名（如 `屈光档案_RG00001234_20261031.pdf`） |
| file_url | VARCHAR(1024) | Y | NULL | 文件地址（对象存储，带时效签名） |
| file_size_bytes | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 文件大小（字节） |
| file_checksum | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 文件 SHA-256 校验值（防篡改与完整性校验） |
| expire_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 文件过期时间（默认生成后 7 天自动清理） |
| download_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 下载次数 |
| last_download_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 最近一次下载时间 |
| **操作者与审批组** | | | | |
| requested_by | BIGINT | N | — | 申请人 ID（M01-02，只读引用） |
| requested_by_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 申请人姓名快照 |
| requested_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 申请时间 |
| approval_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 审批单号（`scope_type = 'ALL'` 或含敏感字段时必填） |
| watermark_text | VARCHAR(128) | Y | NULL | 水印文本（如 `内部资料 仅限诊疗使用 张医师 2026-10-31`） |
| ip_address | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 申请端 IP |
| **标准字段** | | | | |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP（触发器维护） | 更新时间 |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**索引：**

```sql
-- 任务号唯一
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m11_refractive_export_task_no
    ON m11_refractive_export_task (task_no) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按申请人查我的任务（P07 任务列表主力索引）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_export_task_requester
    ON m11_refractive_export_task (requested_by, requested_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按状态查排队/运行中任务（工作进程拉取）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_export_task_status
    ON m11_refractive_export_task (task_status, requested_at ASC)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE AND task_status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING');

-- 按档案查打印历史（P07 单档打印记录）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_export_task_record
    ON m11_refractive_export_task (record_id, requested_at DESC)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE AND record_id IS NOT NULL;

-- 按院区 + 类型统计导出量
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_export_task_org
    ON m11_refractive_export_task (org_id, task_type, requested_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 文件过期清理扫描
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_export_task_expire
    ON m11_refractive_export_task (expire_at)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE AND task_status = 'DONE' AND expire_at IS NOT NULL;

-- 失败任务重试
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_refractive_export_task_failed
    ON m11_refractive_export_task (task_status, finished_at DESC) WHERE task_status = 'FAILED';
```

**约束：**

```sql
-- 任务号格式：DC + 8 位日期 + 4 位流水
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_no_format
    CHECK (task_no ~ '^DC[0-9]{12}$');

-- 任务类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_type
    CHECK (task_type IN ('RECORD_PDF', 'RECORD_PRINT', 'BATCH_EXPORT',
                         'SURGERY_HISTORY_EXPORT', 'BASELINE_EXPORT', 'QUALITY_EXPORT'));

-- 范围类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_scope
    CHECK (scope_type IN ('SINGLE', 'MULTI', 'ALL'));

-- 单档任务必须有档案 ID
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_single
    CHECK (scope_type <> 'SINGLE' OR (record_id IS NOT NULL AND patient_id IS NOT NULL));

-- 多档/全量任务必须保存筛选条件
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_filter
    CHECK (scope_type = 'SINGLE' OR filter_json IS NOT NULL);

-- 全量导出必须有审批单号（合规）
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_approval
    CHECK (scope_type <> 'ALL' OR approval_no IS NOT NULL);

-- 文件格式枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_format
    CHECK (export_format IN ('PDF', 'XLSX', 'CSV', 'JSON'));

-- 任务状态枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_status
    CHECK (task_status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING', 'DONE', 'FAILED', 'EXPIRED', 'CANCELLED'));

-- 进度百分比 0~100
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_progress
    CHECK (progress_pct BETWEEN 0 AND 100);

-- 已完成必须有文件地址与完成时间
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_done
    CHECK (task_status <> 'DONE'
           OR (file_url IS NOT NULL AND finished_at IS NOT NULL
               AND file_size_bytes IS NOT NULL AND expire_at IS NOT NULL));

-- 失败必须有错误信息
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_failed
    CHECK (task_status <> 'FAILED'
           OR (error_message IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(error_message)) >= 5));

-- 重试次数非负且有上限
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_retry
    CHECK (retry_count >= 0 AND retry_count <= 5);

-- 下载次数非负
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_download
    CHECK (download_count >= 0);

-- 文件大小非负
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_size
    CHECK (file_size_bytes IS NULL OR file_size_bytes >= 0);

-- 完成时间不得早于开始时间
ALTER TABLE m11_refractive_export_task
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_refractive_export_task_time_order
    CHECK (finished_at IS NULL OR started_at IS NULL OR finished_at >= started_at);

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_refractive_export_task IS '档案打印与导出任务表：异步生成单档病程 PDF 与批量导出，记录文件校验值与过期时间，满足导出留痕与隐私合规';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_export_task.task_type IS '任务类型：单档 PDF / 档案打印 / 批量导出 / 手术史导出 / 基线导出 / 质控口径导出';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_export_task.file_checksum IS '文件 SHA-256，用于完整性校验与「导出内容未被篡改」举证';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_export_task.watermark_text IS 'PDF 水印文本（含操作人与时间），用于防止导出件外流的责任追溯';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_export_task.expire_at IS '文件过期时间，默认生成后 7 天，过期任务由定时任务置 EXPIRED 并删除对象存储文件';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_refractive_export_task.approval_no IS '全量导出审批单号，scope_type = ALL 时必填';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么导出必须留痕并落到业务表：** 屈光档案含完整眼健康信息（屈光度、角膜厚度、手术史），属敏感个人信息；批量导出是数据外流的主要风险点，必须能回答「谁在何时导出了多少条、包含哪些筛选条件」。
2. **为什么强制 `file_checksum`：** 打印件常作为患者持有的病程证明，一旦发生医疗争议，需要证明「导出的原始内容」与患者手中的纸件一致；SHA-256 校验值是唯一可举证的锚点。
3. **为什么 `scope_type = 'ALL'` 需要审批：** 全量导出等同于数据出境风险（可还原全院屈光手术人群画像），必须以审批单号约束，且由 M20 审批流程留档。
4. **为什么文件 7 天过期：** 对象存储成本与隐私暴露面随时间扩大；7 天足够患者复诊期间多次打印，超期后如需再次获取可重新生成（生成过程本身也是留痕）。
5. **为什么水印文本要存库：** 若只在渲染时拼接水印而不记录，事后无法确认某一版导出件上的水印内容，举证链断裂；入库后可与 `file_checksum` 一起构成完整的「哪一份文件由谁导出」证据。

**分区策略：** 无（6,000/年）。

---

> **4.2~4.10 共 9 张表定义完毕。** 4.11 为 SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型定义，4.12 为 Pydantic Schema 定义。

---

### 4.11 SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型定义

```python
# app/models/m11/refractive_record.py
"""M11-03 屈光档案 —— SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型

9 张表全部以 m11_refractive_ 为前缀。
跨模块引用（M03 患者、M10 验光、M11-02 术前检查、M09 手术记录）一律
只保存业务号指针，不建物理外键，由应用层校验并在每日对账任务中核对。
"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Identity, Index,
    Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger, String, UniqueConstraint, func, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.models.base import Base


class M11RefractiveRecord(Base):
    """屈光档案主表：一患一档、跨年度；只存 M10 验光单指针，不建第二套验光表。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_record"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)

    # ---------- 标识组 ----------
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), comment="屈光档案号 RG+8 位流水，草稿阶段为空")
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False, comment="患者 ID")
    patient_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False, comment="姓名快照")
    gender: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), comment="性别快照")
    birth_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, comment="出生日期快照")
    age_at_first_visit: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, comment="首次建档年龄")
    id_card_mask: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="证件号脱敏值")
    phone_mask: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="电话脱敏值")
    empi_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="EMPI 主索引号")
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, default=1, nullable=False, comment="院区 ID")

    # ---------- 档案状态与归属组 ----------
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="ACTIVE", nullable=False)
    record_level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="STANDARD", nullable=False)
    manage_doctor_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    manage_doctor_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    consult_doctor_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    dept_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    opened_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    closed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    close_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    merged_into_record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    merged_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    # ---------- 屈光概况组 ----------
    refractive_type_dominant: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    sphere_od_avg: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="右眼球镜 D")
    sphere_os_avg: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="左眼球镜 D")
    cyl_od_avg: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="右眼柱镜 D")
    cyl_os_avg: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="左眼柱镜 D")
    spherical_equiv_max: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    is_high_myopia: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    is_prior_refractive_surgery: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    prior_surgery_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, default=0, nullable=False)

    # ---------- 基线与检查指针组（只读引用） ----------
    latest_baseline_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    latest_baseline_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    latest_exam_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="M11-02 检查单号")
    latest_admission_result: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    last_optometry_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), comment="最近验光单号指针，验光数据权威源在 M10-01")
    last_optometry_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    last_vision_summary_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    # ---------- 角膜条件组（μm / mm / D） ----------
    cct_od_min: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="右眼 CCT 最小 μm")
    cct_os_min: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="左眼 CCT 最小 μm")
    keratometry_max: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="最大曲率 D")
    corneal_astig_max: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    axial_length_max: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="最大眼轴 mm")
    endothelial_min: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(8, 2))
    has_thin_cornea: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_irregular_topo: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    # ---------- 标签与备注组 ----------
    tags: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    risk_flags: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))

    # ---------- 标准字段 ----------
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    # ---------- 关系 ----------
    baselines: Mapped[List["M11RefractiveBaseline"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="record", lazy="selectin")
    surgery_histories: Mapped[List["M11RefractiveSurgeryHistory"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="record", lazy="selectin")
    timeline_events: Mapped[List["M11RefractiveTimelineEvent"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="record", lazy="selectin")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_record_no", "record_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND record_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_record_patient_active", "patient_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND status = 'ACTIVE'")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_record_patient", "patient_id", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_record_list", "org_id", "status", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_record_optometry", "last_optometry_no",
              postgresql_where=text("last_optometry_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_record_high_myopia", "is_high_myopia", "org_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_record_merged", "merged_into_record_no",
              postgresql_where=text("merged_into_record_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_record_name_trgm", "patient_name",
              postgresql_using="gin", postgresql_ops={"patient_name": "gin_trgm_ops"}),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_record_tags", "tags",
              postgresql_using="gin", postgresql_ops={"tags": "jsonb_path_ops"}),
        CheckConstraint("record_no IS NULL OR record_no ~ '^RG[0-9]{8}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('ACTIVE', 'CLOSED', 'MERGED')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_status"),
        CheckConstraint("record_level IN ('STANDARD', 'KEY', 'RESEARCH')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_level"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'CLOSED' OR (closed_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND close_reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(close_reason)) >= 5)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_closed"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'MERGED' OR (merged_into_record_no IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND merged_at IS NOT NULL AND merged_into_record_no <> record_no)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_merged"),
        CheckConstraint("(cct_od_min IS NULL OR (cct_od_min BETWEEN 300 AND 900)) "
                        "AND (cct_os_min IS NULL OR (cct_os_min BETWEEN 300 AND 900))",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_cct"),
        CheckConstraint("axial_length_max IS NULL OR (axial_length_max BETWEEN 18 AND 35)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_axial"),
        CheckConstraint("prior_surgery_count >= 0 "
                        "AND (is_prior_refractive_surgery = (prior_surgery_count > 0))",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_prior"),
        CheckConstraint("spherical_equiv_max IS NULL "
                        "OR is_high_myopia = (spherical_equiv_max <= -6.00)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_record_high_myopia"),
        {"comment": "屈光档案主表"},
    )

    # ---------- 领域方法 ----------
    STATUS_LABELS = {"ACTIVE": "在管", "CLOSED": "已结档", "MERGED": "已合并"}
    LEVEL_LABELS = {"STANDARD": "常规", "KEY": "重点关注", "RESEARCH": "科研"}

    @property
    def status_label(self) -> str:
        """档案状态中文名。"""
        return self.STATUS_LABELS.get(self.status, self.status)

    @property
    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        """是否在管（可继续写入基线、方案、随访）。"""
        return self.status == "ACTIVE" and not self.is_deleted

    @property
    def is_high_myopia_calc(self) -> bool:
        """按等效球镜绝对值判定高度近视（阈值 ≥ 6.00D）。"""
        if self.spherical_equiv_max is None:
            return False
        return abs(Decimal(self.spherical_equiv_max)) >= Decimal("6.00")

    def can_make_plan(self) -> bool:
        """是否可生成手术方案：在管 + 有已确认基线 + 准入非 REJECT。"""
        return (
            self.is_active
            and self.latest_baseline_id is not None
            and self.latest_admission_result in (None, "PASS", "HOLD", "PENDING")
        )

    def to_contract_brief(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-02 / P-03 契约出参（精简，避免 N+1）。"""
        return {
            "record_no": self.record_no,
            "patient_id": self.patient_id,
            "patient_name": self.patient_name,
            "gender": self.gender,
            "age": self.age_at_first_visit,
            "status": self.status,
            "record_level": self.record_level,
            "is_high_myopia": self.is_high_myopia,
            "is_prior_refractive_surgery": self.is_prior_refractive_surgery,
            "prior_surgery_count": self.prior_surgery_count,
            "latest_baseline_id": self.latest_baseline_id,
            "latest_baseline_date": self.latest_baseline_date.isoformat()
            if self.latest_baseline_date else None,
            "latest_admission_result": self.latest_admission_result,
            "last_optometry_no": self.last_optometry_no,
            "cct_od_min": float(self.cct_od_min) if self.cct_od_min is not None else None,
            "cct_os_min": float(self.cct_os_min) if self.cct_os_min is not None else None,
            "axial_length_max": (float(self.axial_length_max)
                                 if self.axial_length_max is not None else None),
        }


class M11RefractiveBaseline(Base):
    """术前基线数据表：每次术前检查一条，为方案与术后评价提供共同基线。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_baseline"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    baseline_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    record_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    baseline_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, default=1, nullable=False)

    source_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="PREOP_EXAM", nullable=False)
    exam_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="M11-02 术前检查单号")
    exam_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    source_optometry_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), comment="屈光度来源的 M10 验光单号，仅指针不复制数据")
    for_plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="关联方案号 M11-04")
    purpose: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="SURGERY_PLANNING", nullable=False)
    measured_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    measured_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    measured_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    device_info: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 屈光测量汇总
    refraction_method: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="AUTO", nullable=False)
    is_cycloplegic: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    sphere_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="右眼球镜 D")
    sphere_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="左眼球镜 D")
    cyl_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="右眼柱镜 D")
    cyl_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="左眼柱镜 D")
    axis_od: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, comment="右眼轴位 °")
    axis_os: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, comment="左眼轴位 °")
    spherical_equiv_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    spherical_equiv_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    # 角膜条件汇总（μm / D / mm）
    cct_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="右眼 CCT μm")
    cct_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="左眼 CCT μm")
    cct_thinnest_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cct_thinnest_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k_flat_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k_steep_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k_flat_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k_steep_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k_unit: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="D", nullable=False)
    corneal_astig_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    corneal_astig_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    axis_k_od: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    axis_k_os: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    axial_length_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="眼轴 mm")
    axial_length_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="眼轴 mm")
    endothelial_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(8, 2))
    endothelial_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(8, 2))
    iop_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), comment="眼压 mmHg")
    iop_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), comment="眼压 mmHg")

    # 地形图与影像
    topo_report_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    topo_pattern: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    topo_ki_value: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    pentacam_report_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    image_urls: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 可行性与判定
    baseline_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="DRAFT", nullable=False)
    is_current: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    surgery_feasibility: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    feasibility_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    contraindication_codes: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    thin_cornea_flag: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    dry_eye_flag: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    confirmed_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    confirmed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    record: Mapped["M11RefractiveRecord"] = relationship(back_populates="baselines")
    eyes: Mapped[List["M11RefractiveBaselineEye"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="baseline", lazy="selectin")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_baseline_no", "baseline_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_baseline_version", "record_id", "baseline_version",
              unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND baseline_status <> 'VOID'")),
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_baseline_current", "record_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND is_current = TRUE")),
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_baseline_exam", "exam_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND exam_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_record", "record_id",
              text("baseline_version DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_patient", "patient_id", text("exam_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_pending", "org_id", "baseline_status",
              text("measured_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND baseline_status = 'DRAFT'")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_optometry", "source_optometry_no",
              postgresql_where=text("source_optometry_no IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("baseline_no ~ '^JX[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("baseline_status IN ('DRAFT', 'CONFIRMED', 'SUPERSEDED', 'VOID')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_status"),
        CheckConstraint("source_type IN ('PREOP_EXAM', 'MANUAL', 'EXTERNAL_IMPORT', "
                        "'POSTOP_BASELINE')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_source"),
        CheckConstraint("source_type <> 'PREOP_EXAM' OR exam_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_exam_required"),
        CheckConstraint("(sphere_od IS NULL OR (sphere_od * 4) = trunc(sphere_od * 4)) "
                        "AND (sphere_os IS NULL OR (sphere_os * 4) = trunc(sphere_os * 4))",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_sphere_step"),
        CheckConstraint("((cyl_od IS NULL OR cyl_od = 0) = (axis_od IS NULL)) "
                        "AND ((cyl_os IS NULL OR cyl_os = 0) = (axis_os IS NULL))",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_axis"),
        CheckConstraint("spherical_equiv_od IS NULL OR sphere_od IS NULL "
                        "OR abs(spherical_equiv_od - (sphere_od + coalesce(cyl_od, 0) / 2)) <= 0.13",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_se_od"),
        CheckConstraint("(cct_od IS NULL OR cct_od BETWEEN 300 AND 900) "
                        "AND (cct_os IS NULL OR cct_os BETWEEN 300 AND 900)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_cct"),
        CheckConstraint("(cct_thinnest_od IS NULL OR cct_od IS NULL "
                        "OR cct_thinnest_od <= cct_od)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_cct_thinnest"),
        CheckConstraint("surgery_feasibility NOT IN ('NOT_FEASIBLE', 'CAUTION') "
                        "OR (feasibility_note IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(feasibility_note)) >= 10)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_feasibility_note"),
        CheckConstraint("baseline_status <> 'CONFIRMED' "
                        "OR (confirmed_by IS NOT NULL AND confirmed_at IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_confirmed"),
        CheckConstraint("is_current = FALSE OR baseline_status = 'CONFIRMED'",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_current_confirmed"),
        {"comment": "术前基线数据表"},
    )

    # ---------- 领域方法 ----------
    @property
    def is_confirmed(self) -> bool:
        """基线是否已确认（未确认不得用于手术方案）。"""
        return self.baseline_status == "CONFIRMED"

    def average_cct(self) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """双眼平均中央角膜厚度（μm），单眼缺失时返回另一眼值。"""
        values = [v for v in (self.cct_od, self.cct_os) if v is not None]
        if not values:
            return None
        return sum(values, Decimal("0")) / Decimal(len(values))

    def min_thinnest_cct(self) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """双眼最薄点角膜厚度的最小值（μm），切削深度计算的安全边界。"""
        values = [v for v in (self.cct_thinnest_od, self.cct_thinnest_os) if v is not None]
        return min(values) if values else None

    def evaluate_feasibility(self) -> str:
        """按阈值给出基线层面的术式可行性初判（详细判定归 M11-04）。"""
        thinnest = self.min_thinnest_cct()
        if thinnest is not None and thinnest < Decimal("480"):
            return "NOT_FEASIBLE"
        if self.topo_pattern == "SUSPECT_KERATOCONUS":
            return "NOT_FEASIBLE"
        if thinnest is not None and thinnest < Decimal("500"):
            return "CAUTION"
        return "FEASIBLE"

    def to_brief(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """契约出参：基线摘要。"""
        return {
            "baseline_no": self.baseline_no,
            "baseline_version": self.baseline_version,
            "baseline_status": self.baseline_status,
            "exam_date": self.exam_date.isoformat() if self.exam_date else None,
            "sphere_od": float(self.sphere_od) if self.sphere_od is not None else None,
            "sphere_os": float(self.sphere_os) if self.sphere_os is not None else None,
            "cyl_od": float(self.cyl_od) if self.cyl_od is not None else None,
            "cyl_os": float(self.cyl_os) if self.cyl_os is not None else None,
            "cct_od": float(self.cct_od) if self.cct_od is not None else None,
            "cct_os": float(self.cct_os) if self.cct_os is not None else None,
            "surgery_feasibility": self.surgery_feasibility,
        }


class M11RefractiveBaselineEye(Base):
    """术前基线眼别明细表：按 OD/OS 逐眼记录屈光、曲率、厚度、眼轴、内皮与地形图。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_baseline_eye"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    baseline_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    baseline_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    record_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False, comment="OD 或 OS")

    # 屈光参数
    sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="球镜 D")
    cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="柱镜 D")
    axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, comment="轴位 °")
    spherical_equiv: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    add_power: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    ucva: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(4, 2), comment="裸眼视力")
    bcva: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(4, 2), comment="最佳矫正视力")
    bcva_logmar: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    refraction_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    # 角膜曲率
    k1_value: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k1_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    k2_value: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k2_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    k_mean: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k_mean_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    corneal_astig: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    astig_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))

    # 角膜厚度（μm）
    cct: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="中央角膜厚度 μm")
    cct_thinnest: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="最薄点 μm")
    cct_thinnest_x: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cct_thinnest_y: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cct_superior: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cct_inferior: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cct_nasal: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cct_temporal: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    # 眼轴与眼前段
    axial_length: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="眼轴 mm")
    acd: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), comment="前房深度 mm")
    lens_thickness: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    white_to_white: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    iop: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), comment="眼压 mmHg")

    # 角膜内皮
    endothelial_density: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(8, 2))
    endothelial_cv: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    endothelial_hexagonality: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))

    # 地形图逐眼参数
    topo_ki: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    topo_ihd: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    topo_pattern: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    topo_report_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 术前干眼与眼表
    but_seconds: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 1), comment="BUT 秒")
    schirmer_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 1), comment="泪液分泌 mm")
    tear_film_height: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    meibomian_grade: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)

    # 可行性与风险
    resection_available: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    rsb_estimate: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="预估 RSB μm")
    eye_feasibility: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    eye_contraindication_codes: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    eye_remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))

    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    baseline: Mapped["M11RefractiveBaseline"] = relationship(back_populates="eyes")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_baseline_eye", "baseline_id", "eye_side", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_baseline", "baseline_id", "eye_side",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_record", "record_id", "eye_side",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_patient", "patient_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_thin", "patient_id",
              postgresql_where=text("cct < 480 AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint("sphere IS NULL OR (sphere * 4) = trunc(sphere * 4)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_sphere_step"),
        CheckConstraint("((cylinder IS NULL OR cylinder = 0) = (axis IS NULL)) "
                        "AND (axis IS NULL OR (axis BETWEEN 1 AND 180))",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_axis"),
        CheckConstraint("spherical_equiv IS NULL OR sphere IS NULL "
                        "OR abs(spherical_equiv - (sphere + coalesce(cylinder, 0) / 2)) <= 0.13",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_se"),
        CheckConstraint("(ucva IS NULL OR ucva BETWEEN 0 AND 2) "
                        "AND (bcva IS NULL OR bcva BETWEEN 0 AND 2) "
                        "AND (ucva IS NULL OR bcva IS NULL OR bcva >= ucva)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_va"),
        CheckConstraint("(cct IS NULL OR cct BETWEEN 300 AND 900) "
                        "AND (cct_thinnest IS NULL OR cct_thinnest BETWEEN 300 AND 900) "
                        "AND (cct_thinnest IS NULL OR cct IS NULL OR cct_thinnest <= cct)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_cct"),
        CheckConstraint("axial_length IS NULL OR axial_length BETWEEN 18 AND 35",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_axial"),
        CheckConstraint("rsb_estimate IS NULL OR rsb_estimate >= 0",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_rsb"),
        {"comment": "术前基线眼别明细表"},
    )

    @property
    def effective_cct(self) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """保守取值：有最薄点时用最薄点厚度（μm），否则用中央厚度。"""
        return self.cct_thinnest if self.cct_thinnest is not None else self.cct

    def is_thin_cornea(self, threshold: Decimal = Decimal("480")) -> bool:
        """是否角膜偏薄（默认阈值 480μm）。"""
        value = self.effective_cct
        return value is not None and value < threshold

    def has_dry_eye(self) -> bool:
        """是否干眼（BUT < 5s 或 Schirmer < 5mm）。"""
        but = self.but_seconds
        schirmer = self.schirmer_mm
        return bool((but is not None and but < Decimal("5"))
                    or (schirmer is not None and schirmer < Decimal("5")))


class M11RefractiveSurgeryHistory(Base):
    """历史手术史表：本院（事件落库）+ 外院（人工登记）屈光手术，术前评估硬性前置条件。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_surgery_history"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    record_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    surgery_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, default=1, nullable=False)

    # 手术来源
    source_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="OUR_HOSPITAL", nullable=False)
    surgery_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    date_precision: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="DAY", nullable=False)
    hospital_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(200), comment="外院名称")
    surgeon_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    surgery_record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), comment="本院手术记录号，M09 只读引用")
    reservation_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="M11-05 预约号")
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="M11-04 方案号")
    record_source_doc: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 术式
    refractive_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(24), comment="术式，M01-07 3.1.4 的 10 个码之一")
    refractive_type_label: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    surgery_category: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), default="CORNEAL_LASER", nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="OU", nullable=False)
    flap_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    flap_thickness: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="瓣厚 μm")
    flap_diameter: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    hinge_position: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    optical_zone: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), comment="光学区 mm")

    # 术前与目标
    preop_sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    preop_cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    preop_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    preop_cct: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="术前 CCT μm")
    target_sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    target_cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    planned_cut_depth: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="切削深度 μm")

    # 植入物
    implant_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    implant_model: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    implant_power: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="植入物度数 D")
    implant_serial_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), comment="序列号/UDI")
    implant_position: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))

    # 结果与安全性
    outcome_result: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    postop_sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    postop_cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    postop_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    postop_ucva: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(4, 2))
    postop_cct: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), comment="术后 CCT μm")
    complication_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    retreatment_flag: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    # 核实与记录
    verified_flag: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    verified_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    verified_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    verify_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    recorded_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    recorded_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)

    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    record: Mapped["M11RefractiveRecord"] = relationship(back_populates="surgery_histories")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_surgery_history_seq", "record_id", "surgery_seq",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_surgery_history_dedup",
              "record_id", "eye_side", "surgery_date", "refractive_type", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND surgery_date IS NOT NULL "
                                    "AND refractive_type IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_record", "record_id",
              text("surgery_date DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_patient", "patient_id",
              text("surgery_date DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_unverified", "record_id",
              text("recorded_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND source_type = 'OTHER_HOSPITAL' "
                                    "AND verified_flag = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_type", "refractive_type",
              text("surgery_date DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_implant", "implant_serial_no",
              postgresql_where=text("implant_serial_no IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("source_type IN ('OUR_HOSPITAL', 'OTHER_HOSPITAL', 'PATIENT_REPORT')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_surgery_history_source"),
        CheckConstraint("refractive_type IS NULL OR refractive_type IN "
                        "('SMILE', 'FS_LASIK', 'TRANSPRK', 'SMART', 'ICL', 'PRL', "
                        "'CXL', 'PIOL', 'RK', 'OTHER_REFRACTIVE')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_surgery_history_type"),
        CheckConstraint("source_type <> 'OTHER_HOSPITAL' OR (hospital_name IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(hospital_name)) >= 2)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_surgery_history_hospital"),
        CheckConstraint("source_type <> 'OUR_HOSPITAL' OR surgery_record_no IS NOT NULL "
                        "OR reservation_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_surgery_history_our"),
        CheckConstraint("surgery_category <> 'INTRAOCULAR' OR (implant_type IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND implant_power IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_surgery_history_intraocular"),
        CheckConstraint("outcome_result <> 'COMPLICATION' OR (complication_note IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(complication_note)) >= 5)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_surgery_history_complication"),
        CheckConstraint("preop_cct IS NULL OR postop_cct IS NULL OR postop_cct <= preop_cct",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_surgery_history_cct_order"),
        {"comment": "历史手术史表"},
    )

    # ---------- 领域方法 ----------
    REFRACTIVE_TYPE_LABELS = {
        "SMILE": "飞秒激光小切口角膜基质透镜取出术",
        "FS_LASIK": "飞秒激光制瓣 LASIK",
        "TRANSPRK": "经上皮准分子激光角膜切削术",
        "SMART": "智能脉冲技术表面切削",
        "ICL": "有晶体眼后房型人工晶体植入术",
        "PRL": "后房型悬浮人工晶体植入术",
        "CXL": "角膜胶原交联术",
        "PIOL": "有晶体眼人工晶体植入术",
        "RK": "放射状角膜切开术",
        "OTHER_REFRACTIVE": "其他屈光手术",
    }

    @property
    def type_label(self) -> str:
        """术式中文名（优先快照，其次内置字典）。"""
        if self.refractive_type_label:
            return self.refractive_type_label
        return self.REFRACTIVE_TYPE_LABELS.get(self.refractive_type or "", "")

    @property
    def is_prior_laser(self) -> bool:
        """是否既往角膜激光手术（再次激光手术的核心禁忌判定输入）。"""
        return self.refractive_type in ("SMILE", "FS_LASIK", "TRANSPRK", "SMART", "RK")

    @property
    def is_prior_intraocular(self) -> bool:
        """是否既往眼内植入手术（影响 ICL 选型与拱高评估）。"""
        return self.refractive_type in ("ICL", "PRL", "PIOL")

    def days_since_surgery(self, today: Optional[date] = None) -> Optional[int]:
        """距手术日的天数（稳定期判断用：屈光术后 3~6 个月为稳定期）。"""
        if self.surgery_date is None or self.date_precision != "DAY":
            return None
        return ((today or date.today()) - self.surgery_date).days


class M11RefractiveMedicalHistory(Base):
    """既往史与全身情况表：动态版本化采集，服务于准入、方案风险与并发症归因。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_medical_history"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    record_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    history_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, default=1, nullable=False)
    is_current: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    collected_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    collected_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    collected_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    for_exam_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="服务对象检查单号")

    # 全身疾病
    systemic_diseases: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    has_diabetes: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_hypertension: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_autoimmune: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_collagen_disease: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_glaucoma: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_keratoconus: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_herpes_keratitis: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_ocular_trauma: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    has_amblyopia: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    # 用药与过敏
    on_anticoagulant: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    anticoagulant_detail: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    on_isotretinoin: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    on_corticosteroid: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    allergy_drugs: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    allergy_materials: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    allergy_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))

    # 眼部与屈光相关
    contact_lens_wear: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    contact_lens_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    last_lens_off_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, comment="末次摘镜日期")
    contact_lens_years: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 1))
    dry_eye_diagnosed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    amblyopia_eye: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8))

    # 家族与生活方式
    family_myopia: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    family_keratoconus: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    is_pregnant: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    is_lactating: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    smoking: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    occupation_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 手术安全性结论
    contraindication_hit: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    contraindication_codes: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    risk_level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="LOW", nullable=False)
    risk_factors: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    history_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))

    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_medical_history_current", "record_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND is_current = TRUE")),
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_medical_history_version", "record_id", "history_version",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_medical_history_record", "record_id",
              text("history_version DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_medical_history_patient", "patient_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_medical_history_contra", "patient_id",
              postgresql_where=text("contraindication_hit = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_medical_history_risk", "risk_level",
              postgresql_where=text("risk_level IN ('MEDIUM', 'HIGH')")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_medical_history_diseases", "systemic_diseases",
              postgresql_using="gin",
              postgresql_ops={"systemic_diseases": "jsonb_path_ops"}),
        CheckConstraint("contact_lens_wear = FALSE OR (contact_lens_type IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND last_lens_off_date IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_medical_history_lens_wear"),
        CheckConstraint("on_anticoagulant = FALSE OR (anticoagulant_detail IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(anticoagulant_detail)) >= 2)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_medical_history_anticoagulant"),
        CheckConstraint("risk_level IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_medical_history_risk"),
        CheckConstraint("risk_level <> 'HIGH' OR risk_factors IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_medical_history_risk_factors"),
        CheckConstraint("contraindication_hit = FALSE OR contraindication_codes IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_medical_history_contra"),
        CheckConstraint("(is_pregnant = FALSE AND is_lactating = FALSE) "
                        "OR contraindication_hit = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_medical_history_pregnant"),
        CheckConstraint("history_version >= 1",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_medical_history_version"),
        {"comment": "既往史与全身情况表"},
    )

    # ---------- 领域方法 ----------
    HARD_CONTRAINDICATIONS = ("has_keratoconus", "is_pregnant", "is_lactating")

    def collect_hard_contraindications(self) -> List[str]:
        """返回命中的硬禁忌字段名列表。"""
        return [name for name in self.HARD_CONTRAINDICATIONS if getattr(self, name)]

    def lens_stop_days_required(self) -> Optional[int]:
        """按接触镜类型返回术前所需停戴天数。"""
        mapping = {"SOFT": 14, "RGP": 28, "ORTHO_K": 28}
        return mapping.get(self.contact_lens_type or "")

    def lens_stop_compliant(self, exam_date: Optional[date] = None) -> bool:
        """判断停戴期是否满足要求（未配戴接触镜视为合规）。"""
        if not self.contact_lens_wear:
            return True
        required = self.lens_stop_days_required()
        if required is None or self.last_lens_off_date is None:
            return False
        reference = exam_date or date.today()
        return (reference - self.last_lens_off_date).days >= required


class M11RefractiveTimelineEvent(Base):
    """档案时间轴事件表：事件流串联 M11 全子模块与 M09/M10 节点，append-only。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_timeline_event"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    record_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    event_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, nullable=False)

    event_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    event_category: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="CLINICAL", nullable=False)
    event_label: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128))
    event_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False,
        comment="事件时间，时间轴排序键")
    event_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)

    # 业务关联指针（只读引用，不建外键）
    ref_module: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    ref_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    ref_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    consult_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    exam_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    baseline_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    reservation_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    followup_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    complication_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    surgery_record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="M09 手术记录号")
    optometry_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), comment="M10-01 验光单号")

    # 事件内容
    eye_side: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8))
    refractive_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    summary: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    key_metrics: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    detail_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 展示与追溯
    is_key_node: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    display_order: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    source_event_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    idempotency_key: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128))
    recorded_offline: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, default=1, nullable=False)

    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False,
        primary_key=True, comment="分区键")
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    record: Mapped["M11RefractiveRecord"] = relationship(back_populates="timeline_events")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_timeline_event_idem", "idempotency_key", "created_at",
              unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND idempotency_key IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_record", "record_id",
              text("event_at DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_key", "record_id", text("event_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND is_key_node = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_type", "event_type",
              text("event_at DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_patient", "patient_id",
              text("event_at DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_ref", "ref_module", "ref_no",
              postgresql_where=text("ref_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_org", "org_id", text("event_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("event_type IN ('RECORD_CREATED', 'CONSULT_CREATED', "
                        "'PREOP_EXAM_DONE', 'BASELINE_CONFIRMED', 'PLAN_MADE', "
                        "'PLAN_AUDITED', 'RESERVATION_CREATED', 'SURGERY_DONE', "
                        "'FOLLOWUP_DONE', 'COMPLICATION_REGISTERED', 'RETREATMENT', "
                        "'RECORD_MERGED', 'RECORD_CLOSED', 'OPTIMETRY_IMPORTED')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_timeline_event_type"),
        CheckConstraint("event_category IN ('CLINICAL', 'BUSINESS', 'SYSTEM', 'GOVERNANCE')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_timeline_event_category"),
        CheckConstraint("event_type <> 'OPTIMETRY_IMPORTED' OR optometry_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_timeline_event_optometry"),
        CheckConstraint("event_category <> 'CLINICAL' OR ref_no IS NOT NULL "
                        "OR surgery_record_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_timeline_event_ref"),
        CheckConstraint("event_seq >= 1", name="ck_m11_refractive_timeline_event_seq"),
        {
            "comment": "档案时间轴事件表",
            "postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (created_at)",
        },
    )

    # ---------- 领域方法 ----------
    @staticmethod
    def build_idempotency_key(ref_module: str, ref_no: str, event_type: str) -> str:
        """构造幂等键：模块 + 单据号 + 事件类型，防跨模块事件重复落库。"""
        return f"{ref_module}:{ref_no}:{event_type}"

    @property
    def is_governance(self) -> bool:
        """是否治理类事件（合并/纠错/结档），时间轴上以不同颜色区分。"""
        return self.event_category == "GOVERNANCE"

    def to_card(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """时间轴卡片出参。"""
        return {
            "event_seq": self.event_seq,
            "event_type": self.event_type,
            "event_label": self.event_label,
            "event_at": self.event_at.isoformat() if self.event_at else None,
            "eye_side": self.eye_side,
            "summary": self.summary,
            "key_metrics": self.key_metrics,
            "ref_module": self.ref_module,
            "ref_no": self.ref_no,
            "is_key_node": self.is_key_node,
            "recorded_offline": self.recorded_offline,
        }


class M11RefractiveMergeLog(Base):
    """档案合并与纠错记录表：合并/拆分/字段纠错全过程，含审批双人留痕。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_merge_log"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    merge_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    merge_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="MERGE", nullable=False)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, default=1, nullable=False)

    source_record_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    source_record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    source_patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    target_record_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    target_record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    target_patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)

    moved_surgery_history_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    moved_medical_history_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
```
