```python
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_merge_log_no", "merge_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_merge_log_source", "source_record_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND merge_type = 'MERGE' "
                                    "AND exec_status = 'DONE'")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_merge_log_target", "target_record_id",
              text("apply_at DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_merge_log_pending", "org_id", "exec_status",
              text("apply_at ASC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND exec_status = 'PENDING'")),
        CheckConstraint("merge_no ~ '^BH[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("merge_type IN ('MERGE', 'SPLIT', 'CORRECT', 'REDIRECT')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_type"),
        CheckConstraint("merge_type NOT IN ('MERGE', 'REDIRECT') "
                        "OR (source_record_id IS NOT NULL AND source_record_no IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_source"),
        CheckConstraint("source_record_id IS NULL OR target_record_id <> source_record_id",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_not_self"),
        CheckConstraint("merge_type <> 'CORRECT' OR (target_table IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND target_row_id IS NOT NULL AND target_field IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND old_value IS NOT NULL AND new_value IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND correct_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(correct_reason)) >= 10)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_correct"),
        CheckConstraint("exec_status IN ('PENDING', 'EXECUTING', 'DONE', 'FAILED', 'REJECTED')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_exec"),
        CheckConstraint("audit_by IS NULL OR audit_by <> apply_by",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_self_audit"),
        CheckConstraint("exec_status <> 'DONE' OR exec_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_done"),
        CheckConstraint("rollback_flag = FALSE OR (rollback_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND rollback_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(rollback_reason)) >= 5)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_rollback"),
        CheckConstraint("char_length(btrim(apply_reason)) >= 10",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_merge_log_apply_reason"),
        {"comment": "档案合并与纠错记录表"},
    )

    # ---------- 领域方法 ----------
    @property
    def needs_audit(self) -> bool:
        """是否需要双人复核（合并、拆分、以及影响方案的纠错都需要）。"""
        return self.merge_type in ("MERGE", "SPLIT") or self.impact_scope == "PLAN_AFFECTED"

    @property
    def can_rollback(self, now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> bool:
        """是否仍可回滚（执行成功后 24 小时内且未回滚过）。"""
        if self.exec_status != "DONE" or self.rollback_flag or self.exec_at is None:
            return False
        reference = now or datetime.now()
        return (reference - self.exec_at).total_seconds() <= 24 * 3600

    def total_moved_rows(self) -> int:
        """合并迁移的总行数（用于回滚前的一致性校验）。"""
        return (self.moved_baseline_count + self.moved_baseline_eye_count
                + self.moved_surgery_history_count + self.moved_medical_history_count
                + self.moved_timeline_event_count)


class M11RefractiveChangeLog(Base):
    """档案变更审计日志表：全部写操作的字段级变更，append-only，月度分区。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_refractive_change_log"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_change_log_no", "log_no", "created_at", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_change_log_record", "record_id",
              text("created_at DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_change_log_object", "object_type", "object_id",
              text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND object_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_change_log_sensitive", "record_id",
              text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND sensitive_field_hit = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_change_log_operator", "operator_id",
              text("created_at DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("log_no ~ '^CL[0-9]{14}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_change_log_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("object_type IN ('RECORD', 'BASELINE', 'BASELINE_EYE', "
                        "'SURGERY_HISTORY', 'MEDICAL_HISTORY', 'TIMELINE', 'MERGE', 'EXPORT')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_change_log_object"),
        CheckConstraint("action IN ('CREATE', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'CONFIRM', 'SUBMIT', "
                        "'AUDIT', 'REJECT', 'MERGE', 'CORRECT', 'CLOSE', 'REOPEN', "
                        "'IMPORT', 'EXPORT', 'PRINT', 'SYNC')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_change_log_action"),
        CheckConstraint("action <> 'UPDATE' OR (changed_fields IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND change_summary IS NOT NULL AND field_count IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND field_count > 0)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_change_log_update"),
        CheckConstraint("sensitive_field_hit = FALSE OR (reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(reason)) >= 5)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_change_log_sensitive_reason"),
        CheckConstraint("operator_role = 'SYSTEM' "
                        "OR (operator_id IS NOT NULL AND operator_name IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_change_log_operator"),
        {
            "comment": "档案变更审计日志表",
            "postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (created_at)",
        },
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    log_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    record_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, default=1, nullable=False)

    object_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), default="RECORD", nullable=False)
    object_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    object_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    action_label: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    from_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    to_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))

    changed_fields: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    change_summary: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    field_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    sensitive_field_hit: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))

    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    operator_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    client_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    ip_address: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    request_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False,
        primary_key=True, comment="分区键")
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    # ---------- 常量与方法 ----------
    SENSITIVE_FIELDS = frozenset({
        "sphere", "sphere_od", "sphere_os", "cylinder", "cyl_od", "cyl_os",
        "axis", "axis_od", "axis_os", "cct", "cct_od", "cct_os",
        "cct_thinnest", "cct_thinnest_od", "cct_thinnest_os",
        "k1_value", "k2_value", "axial_length", "axial_length_od", "axial_length_os",
        "refractive_type", "surgery_date", "preop_sphere", "outcome_result",
    })

    @classmethod
    def detect_sensitive(cls, changed_fields: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """判断变更是否涉及临床关键字段（屈光度/厚度/轴位/手术史）。"""
        return any(key in cls.SENSITIVE_FIELDS for key in changed_fields.keys())

    @staticmethod
    def build_diff(old: Dict[str, Any], new: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """生成字段级差异：仅保留发生变化的字段，结构 {字段: {old, new}}。"""
        diff: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key, new_value in new.items():
            old_value = old.get(key)
            if old_value != new_value:
                diff[key] = {"old": old_value, "new": new_value}
        return diff


class M11RefractiveExportTask(Base):
    """档案打印与导出任务表：异步生成 PDF / 批量导出，导出留痕与隐私合规。"""

    task_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="PENDING", nullable=False)

    started_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    finished_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    error_message: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    retry_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, default=0, nullable=False)

    file_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    file_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    file_size_bytes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    file_checksum: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), comment="SHA-256")
    expire_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    download_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, default=0, nullable=False)
    last_download_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    requested_by: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    requested_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    requested_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    watermark_text: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128))
    ip_address: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.current_timestamp(), nullable=False)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_refractive_export_task_no", "task_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_export_task_requester", "requested_by",
              text("requested_at DESC"), postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_export_task_status", "task_status",
              text("requested_at ASC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND task_status IN "
                                    "('PENDING', 'RUNNING')")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_export_task_record", "record_id",
              text("requested_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND record_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_refractive_export_task_expire", "expire_at",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND task_status = 'DONE' "
                                    "AND expire_at IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("task_no ~ '^DC[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("task_type IN ('RECORD_PDF', 'RECORD_PRINT', 'BATCH_EXPORT', "
                        "'SURGERY_HISTORY_EXPORT', 'BASELINE_EXPORT', 'QUALITY_EXPORT')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_type"),
        CheckConstraint("scope_type IN ('SINGLE', 'MULTI', 'ALL')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_scope"),
        CheckConstraint("scope_type <> 'SINGLE' OR (record_id IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND patient_id IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_single"),
        CheckConstraint("scope_type = 'SINGLE' OR filter_json IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_filter"),
        CheckConstraint("scope_type <> 'ALL' OR approval_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_approval"),
        CheckConstraint("task_status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING', 'DONE', 'FAILED', "
                        "'EXPIRED', 'CANCELLED')",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_status"),
        CheckConstraint("progress_pct BETWEEN 0 AND 100",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_progress"),
        CheckConstraint("task_status <> 'DONE' OR (file_url IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND finished_at IS NOT NULL AND file_size_bytes IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND expire_at IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_done"),
        CheckConstraint("retry_count >= 0 AND retry_count <= 5",
                        name="ck_m11_refractive_export_task_retry"),
        {"comment": "档案打印与导出任务表"},
    )

    # ---------- 领域方法 ----------
    def is_expired(self, now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> bool:
        """文件是否已过期（需清理对象存储）。"""
        if self.expire_at is None:
            return False
        return (now or datetime.now()) > self.expire_at

    def build_watermark(self, operator_name: str, org_name: str) -> str:
        """构造 PDF 水印文本：机构 + 用途限制 + 操作人 + 日期。"""
        return (f"{org_name} 内部资料 仅限诊疗使用 "
                f"{operator_name} {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}")

    def can_retry(self) -> bool:
        """是否允许重试（失败且未超重试上限）。"""
        return self.task_status == "FAILED" and self.retry_count < 5
```

---

### 4.12 Pydantic Schema 定义

```python
# app/schemas/m11/refractive_record.py
"""M11-03 屈光档案 —— Pydantic V2 Schema"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


# ==================== 枚举字面量 ====================

RecordStatus = Literal["ACTIVE", "CLOSED", "MERGED"]
RecordLevel = Literal["STANDARD", "KEY", "RESEARCH"]
EyeSide = Literal["OD", "OS", "OU", "NA"]
EyeSideSingle = Literal["OD", "OS"]
RefractiveType = Literal["SMILE", "FS_LASIK", "TRANSPRK", "SMART", "ICL",
                         "PRL", "CXL", "PIOL", "RK", "OTHER_REFRACTIVE"]
BaselineStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "CONFIRMED", "SUPERSEDED", "VOID"]
Feasibility = Literal["FEASIBLE", "CAUTION", "NOT_FEASIBLE", "NEED_MORE_EXAM"]
AdmissionResult = Literal["PASS", "HOLD", "REJECT", "PENDING"]
RiskLevel = Literal["LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH"]
MergeType = Literal["MERGE", "SPLIT", "CORRECT", "REDIRECT"]
ExportFormat = Literal["PDF", "XLSX", "CSV", "JSON"]


# ==================== 公共校验函数 ====================

def check_step_025(value: Optional[Decimal], field_name: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
    """校验屈光度是否为 0.25D 整数倍。"""
    if value is None:
        return value
    scaled = Decimal(value) * 4
    if scaled != scaled.to_integral_value():
        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} 必须为 0.25D 的整数倍，当前 {value}D")
    return Decimal(value)


def check_axis(axis: Optional[int], cylinder: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[int]:
    """柱镜为 0 时不得填轴位；柱镜非 0 时轴位必填且 1~180。"""
    if not cylinder:
        if axis is not None:
            raise ValueError("柱镜为 0 时不可填写轴位")
        return axis
    if axis is None:
        raise ValueError("柱镜不为 0 时必须填写轴位")
    if not (1 <= int(axis) <= 180):
        raise ValueError("轴位必须在 1°~180° 之间")
    return int(axis)


# ==================== 请求体 ====================

class RecordCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """新建屈光档案。"""

    patient_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="患者 ID")
    org_id: int = Field(1, gt=0, description="院区 ID")
    record_level: RecordLevel = Field("STANDARD", description="档案级别")
    manage_doctor_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="主管屈光医师 ID")
    consult_doctor_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="首诊咨询师 ID")
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="归口科室 ID")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")


class RecordUpdateIn(BaseModel):
    """维护屈光档案基本信息。"""

    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")
    record_level: Optional[RecordLevel] = Field(None, description="档案级别")
    manage_doctor_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="主管医师 ID")
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="归口科室 ID")
    tags: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, description="标签数组")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")


class BaselineEyeIn(BaseModel):
    """单眼基线参数录入。"""

    eye_side: EyeSideSingle = Field(..., description="眼别：OD 右眼 / OS 左眼")
    sphere: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="球镜（D），如 -4.25D")
    cylinder: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="柱镜（D），如 -1.25D")
    axis: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="轴位（°），柱镜非 0 时必填")
    ucva: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, le=2, description="裸眼视力（小数记录）")
    bcva: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, le=2, description="最佳矫正视力")
    k1_value: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=35, le=60, description="平曲率 K1（D）")
    k2_value: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=35, le=60, description="陡曲率 K2（D）")
    k1_axis: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=180, description="K1 轴位（°）")
    k2_axis: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=180, description="K2 轴位（°）")
    cct: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=300, le=900, description="中央角膜厚度（μm），如 520μm")
    cct_thinnest: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=300, le=900, description="最薄点厚度（μm）")
    cct_superior: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=300, le=900, description="上方象限厚度（μm）")
    cct_inferior: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=300, le=900, description="下方象限厚度（μm）")
    cct_nasal: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=300, le=900, description="鼻侧象限厚度（μm）")
    cct_temporal: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=300, le=900, description="颞侧象限厚度（μm）")
    axial_length: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=18, le=35, description="眼轴长度（mm）")
    acd: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=1, le=6, description="前房深度（mm）")
    white_to_white: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=8, le=15, description="角膜横径（mm）")
    iop: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=5, le=60, description="眼压（mmHg）")
    endothelial_density: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=500, le=5000,
                                                  description="内皮细胞密度（cells/mm²）")
    but_seconds: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, le=60, description="泪膜破裂时间（秒）")
    schirmer_mm: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, le=40, description="泪液分泌（mm/5min）")
    topo_pattern: Optional[Literal["NORMAL", "SUSPECT_KERATOCONUS",
                                   "IRREGULAR", "ASTIGMATIC"]] = Field(
        None, description="地形图形态")
    eye_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="眼别备注")

    @field_validator("sphere", "cylinder")
    @classmethod
    def validate_step(cls, v: Optional[Decimal], info) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """屈光度必须为 0.25D 整数倍。"""
        return check_step_025(v, info.field_name)

    @field_validator("cylinder")
    @classmethod
    def validate_cyl_negative(cls, v: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """术前统一负柱镜表达。"""
        if v is not None and v > 0:
            raise ValueError("术前屈光统一使用负柱镜，如 -1.25D")
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_eye(self) -> "BaselineEyeIn":
        """跨字段校验：轴位、角膜厚度、视力、曲率顺序。"""
        check_axis(self.axis, self.cylinder)
        if self.cct_thinnest is not None and self.cct is not None and self.cct_thinnest > self.cct:
            raise ValueError("最薄点角膜厚度不得大于中央角膜厚度")
        if self.bcva is not None and self.ucva is not None and self.bcva < self.ucva:
            raise ValueError("最佳矫正视力不得低于裸眼视力")
        if self.k2_value is not None and self.k1_value is not None and self.k2_value < self.k1_value:
            raise ValueError("陡曲率 K2 不得小于平曲率 K1")
        return self


class BaselineCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """创建术前基线（含双眼明细）。"""

    record_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="档案号，如 RG00001234")
    source_type: Literal["PREOP_EXAM", "MANUAL", "EXTERNAL_IMPORT",
                         "POSTOP_BASELINE"] = Field("MANUAL", description="基线来源")
    exam_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="术前检查单号（M11-02）")
    exam_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="检查日期")
    source_optometry_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="屈光度来源验光单号")
    for_plan_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="关联方案号")
    purpose: Literal["SURGERY_PLANNING", "RETREATMENT", "ENHANCEMENT", "RESEARCH"] = Field(
        "SURGERY_PLANNING", description="基线用途")
    refraction_method: Literal["AUTO", "SUBJECTIVE", "CYCLOPLEGIC", "COMBINED"] = Field(
        "AUTO", description="屈光测量方式")
    is_cycloplegic: bool = Field(False, description="是否散瞳后测量")
    eyes: List[BaselineEyeIn] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=2, description="眼别明细")
    image_urls: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, max_length=12, description="影像地址")
    feasibility_note: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="可行性说明")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_baseline(self) -> "BaselineCreateIn":
        """校验来源必填项与眼别唯一性。"""
        if self.source_type == "PREOP_EXAM" and not self.exam_no:
            raise ValueError("来源为术前检查时必须填写检查单号")
        sides = [eye.eye_side for eye in self.eyes]
        if len(sides) != len(set(sides)):
            raise ValueError("同一基线内眼别不能重复（OD 与 OS 各一条）")
        return self


class BaselineConfirmIn(BaseModel):
    """确认基线（草稿 → 已确认），确认后可作为手术方案输入。"""

    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")
    feasibility: Feasibility = Field(..., description="术式可行性初判")
    feasibility_note: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024,
                                            description="可行性说明，不可行/需谨慎时必填")
    confirmed_by: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="确认医师 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_note(self) -> "BaselineConfirmIn":
        """不可行或需谨慎必须写说明（≥ 10 字）。"""
        if self.feasibility in ("NOT_FEASIBLE", "CAUTION"):
            if not self.feasibility_note or len(self.feasibility_note.strip()) < 10:
                raise ValueError("不可行或需谨慎必须填写不少于 10 字的说明")
        return self


class SurgeryHistoryIn(BaseModel):
    """登记历史手术史（含外院）。"""

    record_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="档案号")
    source_type: Literal["OUR_HOSPITAL", "OTHER_HOSPITAL", "PATIENT_REPORT"] = Field(
        ..., description="来源：本院 / 外院 / 患者自述")
    surgery_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="手术日期")
    date_precision: Literal["DAY", "MONTH", "YEAR", "UNKNOWN"] = Field(
        "DAY", description="日期精度")
    hospital_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=200, description="手术医院（外院必填）")
    surgeon_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="主刀医师")
    refractive_type: Optional[RefractiveType] = Field(None, description="术式")
    surgery_category: Literal["CORNEAL_LASER", "INTRAOCULAR", "CORNEAL_OTHER",
                              "COMBINED"] = Field("CORNEAL_LASER", description="手术大类")
    eye_side: EyeSide = Field("OU", description="手术眼别")
    flap_thickness: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=80, le=180, description="瓣厚（μm）")
    optical_zone: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=4.5, le=8.5, description="光学区（mm）")
    preop_sphere: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="术前球镜（D）")
    preop_cylinder: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="术前柱镜（D）")
    preop_axis: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="术前轴位（°）")
    preop_cct: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=300, le=900, description="术前厚度（μm）")
    implant_type: Optional[Literal["ICL", "PRL", "PIOL", "NONE"]] = Field(
        None, description="植入物类型")
    implant_power: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="植入物度数（D）")
    implant_serial_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="植入物序列号")
    outcome_result: Optional[Literal["SUCCESS", "PARTIAL", "FAILED",
                                     "COMPLICATION", "UNKNOWN"]] = Field(
        None, description="手术结果")
    complication_note: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="并发症说明")

    @field_validator("preop_sphere", "preop_cylinder", "implant_power")
    @classmethod
    def validate_step(cls, v: Optional[Decimal], info) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """屈光度必须为 0.25D 整数倍。"""
        return check_step_025(v, info.field_name)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_history(self) -> "SurgeryHistoryIn":
        """外院必填医院；眼内手术必填植入物；并发症必填说明。"""
        if self.source_type == "OTHER_HOSPITAL":
            if not self.hospital_name or len(self.hospital_name.strip()) < 2:
                raise ValueError("外院手术必须填写手术医院名称")
        if self.surgery_category == "INTRAOCULAR" and not self.implant_power:
            raise ValueError("眼内植入手术必须填写植入物度数")
        if self.outcome_result == "COMPLICATION":
            if not self.complication_note or len(self.complication_note.strip()) < 5:
                raise ValueError("发生并发症必须填写不少于 5 字的说明")
        return self


class MedicalHistoryIn(BaseModel):
    """采集既往史与全身情况（生成新版本）。"""

    record_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="档案号")
    for_exam_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="服务对象检查单号")
    systemic_diseases: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, description="全身疾病编码数组")
    has_diabetes: bool = Field(False, description="糖尿病")
    has_hypertension: bool = Field(False, description="高血压")
    has_autoimmune: bool = Field(False, description="自身免疫性疾病")
    has_keratoconus: bool = Field(False, description="圆锥角膜史（绝对禁忌）")
    has_herpes_keratitis: bool = Field(False, description="单纯疱疹病毒性角膜炎史")
    on_anticoagulant: bool = Field(False, description="在用抗凝/抗血小板药")
    anticoagulant_detail: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=256, description="抗凝药明细")
    on_isotretinoin: bool = Field(False, description="在用异维 A 酸")
    contact_lens_wear: bool = Field(False, description="是否配戴过接触镜")
    contact_lens_type: Optional[Literal["SOFT", "RGP", "ORTHO_K", "NONE"]] = Field(
        None, description="接触镜类型")
    last_lens_off_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="末次摘镜日期")
    is_pregnant: bool = Field(False, description="是否怀孕（禁忌）")
    is_lactating: bool = Field(False, description="是否哺乳期（禁忌）")
    smoking: Optional[Literal["NEVER", "OCCASIONAL", "DAILY", "QUIT"]] = Field(
        None, description="吸烟情况")
    occupation_type: Optional[Literal["OFFICE", "DRIVER", "OUTDOOR", "ATHLETE",
                                      "STUDENT", "OTHER"]] = Field(
        None, description="职业类型")
    history_note: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="补充说明")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_medical(self) -> "MedicalHistoryIn":
        """抗凝药必填明细；配戴接触镜必填类型与摘镜日期。"""
        if self.on_anticoagulant and not self.anticoagulant_detail:
            raise ValueError("使用抗凝药必须填写药名与用法")
        if self.contact_lens_wear and (not self.contact_lens_type
                                       or not self.last_lens_off_date):
            raise ValueError("配戴过接触镜必须填写镜片类型与末次摘镜日期")
        return self


class MergeApplyIn(BaseModel):
    """申请档案合并。"""

    source_record_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="被合并（源）档案号")
    target_record_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="目标（保留）档案号")
    apply_reason: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=512, description="申请理由（≥ 10 字）")
    conflict_strategy: Literal["KEEP_TARGET", "KEEP_SOURCE", "MANUAL"] = Field(
        "KEEP_TARGET", description="冲突处理策略（双方均有当前基线时）")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_merge(self) -> "MergeApplyIn":
        """源档案与目标档案不能相同。"""
        if self.source_record_no == self.target_record_no:
            raise ValueError("被合并档案与目标档案不能是同一份")
        return self


class MergeAuditIn(BaseModel):
    """审核合并/纠错申请。"""

    merge_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="合并记录号")
    audit_result: Literal["APPROVED", "REJECTED"] = Field(..., description="审核结论")
    audit_note: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="审核意见，驳回时必填")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_audit(self) -> "MergeAuditIn":
        """驳回必须写审核意见（≥ 5 字）。"""
        if self.audit_result == "REJECTED":
            if not self.audit_note or len(self.audit_note.strip()) < 5:
                raise ValueError("驳回必须填写不少于 5 字的审核意见")
        return self


class CorrectFieldIn(BaseModel):
    """字段级纠错申请。"""

    record_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="档案号")
    target_table: Literal["m11_refractive_record", "m11_refractive_baseline",
                          "m11_refractive_baseline_eye", "m11_refractive_surgery_history",
                          "m11_refractive_medical_history"] = Field(
        ..., description="纠错目标表")
    target_row_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="纠错目标行 ID")
    target_field: str = Field(..., max_length=64, description="纠错字段")
    new_value: str = Field(..., max_length=512, description="修改后的值")
    correct_reason: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=512, description="纠错原因（≥ 10 字）")


class ExportRequestIn(BaseModel):
    """申请打印或导出。"""

    task_type: Literal["RECORD_PDF", "RECORD_PRINT", "BATCH_EXPORT",
                       "SURGERY_HISTORY_EXPORT", "BASELINE_EXPORT",
                       "QUALITY_EXPORT"] = Field(..., description="任务类型")
    record_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="单档档案号")
    scope_type: Literal["SINGLE", "MULTI", "ALL"] = Field("SINGLE", description="范围类型")
    filter_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(None, description="筛选条件（多档/全量）")
    export_format: ExportFormat = Field("PDF", description="文件格式")
    include_images: bool = Field(False, description="是否包含影像")
    template_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="打印模板编码")
    approval_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="审批单号（全量导出必填）")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_export(self) -> "ExportRequestIn":
        """单档必须给档案号；全量必须给审批单号。"""
        if self.scope_type == "SINGLE" and not self.record_no:
            raise ValueError("单档打印/导出必须提供档案号")
        if self.scope_type in ("MULTI", "ALL") and not self.filter_json:
            raise ValueError("多档或全量导出必须提供筛选条件")
        if self.scope_type == "ALL" and not self.approval_no:
            raise ValueError("全量导出必须提供审批单号")
        return self

class RefractiveRecordBriefOut(BaseModel):
    """屈光档案简要出参（列表行）。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    id: int
    record_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="屈光档案号，如 RG00001234")
    patient_id: int = Field(..., description="患者 ID")
    patient_name: str = Field(..., description="患者姓名")
    gender: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="性别")
    age_at_first_visit: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="首次建档年龄")
    status: RecordStatus = Field(..., description="档案状态")
    record_level: RecordLevel = Field(..., description="档案级别")
    is_high_myopia: bool = Field(False, description="是否高度近视")
    is_prior_refractive_surgery: bool = Field(False, description="是否既往屈光手术")
    prior_surgery_count: int = Field(0, description="既往手术次数")
    latest_admission_result: Optional[AdmissionResult] = Field(None, description="最近准入结论")
    latest_baseline_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="最新基线日期")
    cct_od_min: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="右眼最小角膜厚度（μm）")
    cct_os_min: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="左眼最小角膜厚度（μm）")
    created_at: datetime = Field(..., description="建档时间")

class RecordDetailOut(BaseModel):
    """屈光档案详情出参（含基线、手术史、既往史摘要）。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    id: int
    record_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="档案号")
    patient_id: int = Field(..., description="患者 ID")
    patient_name: str = Field(..., description="患者姓名")
    gender: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="性别")
    age_at_first_visit: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="首次建档年龄")
    phone_mask: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="电话脱敏值")
    org_id: int = Field(..., description="院区 ID")
    status: RecordStatus = Field(..., description="档案状态")
    record_level: RecordLevel = Field(..., description="档案级别")
    manage_doctor_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="主管医师")
    opened_at: datetime = Field(..., description="建档时间")
    is_high_myopia: bool = Field(False, description="是否高度近视")
    is_prior_refractive_surgery: bool = Field(False, description="是否既往屈光手术")
    prior_surgery_count: int = Field(0, description="既往手术次数")
    refractive_type_dominant: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="主导屈光类型")
    latest_admission_result: Optional[AdmissionResult] = Field(None, description="最近准入结论")
    last_optometry_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="最近验光单号（M10-01 指针）")
    last_optometry_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="最近验光时间")
    has_thin_cornea: bool = Field(False, description="是否角膜偏薄")
    has_irregular_topo: bool = Field(False, description="是否地形图异常")
    tags: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, description="标签")
    risk_flags: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, description="风险标记")
    current_baseline: Optional[BaselineOut] = Field(None, description="当前生效基线")
    surgery_histories: List[SurgeryHistoryOut] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                                       description="历史手术史")
    medical_history: Optional[MedicalHistoryOut] = Field(None, description="当前既往史")
    version: int = Field(0, description="乐观锁版本号")
    created_at: datetime = Field(..., description="创建时间")


class RecordPageOut(BaseModel):
    """档案分页出参。"""

    items: List[RefractiveRecordBriefOut] = Field(default_factory=list, description="当前页数据")
    total: int = Field(0, description="总条数")
    page: int = Field(1, description="页码")
    page_size: int = Field(20, description="每页条数")
    pages: int = Field(0, description="总页数")


class RecordContractOut(BaseModel):
    """P-02 / P-03 内部契约出参（供 M07 / M12 / M16-04 / M22 消费）。"""

    record_no: str = Field(..., description="屈光档案号")
    patient_id: int = Field(..., description="患者 ID")
    patient_name: str = Field(..., description="患者姓名")
    status: RecordStatus = Field(..., description="档案状态")
    record_level: RecordLevel = Field(..., description="档案级别")
    is_high_myopia: bool = Field(False, description="是否高度近视")
    is_prior_refractive_surgery: bool = Field(False, description="是否既往屈光手术")
    prior_surgery_count: int = Field(0, description="既往手术次数")
    latest_admission_result: Optional[AdmissionResult] = Field(None, description="最近准入结论")
    latest_baseline_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="最新基线日期")
    last_optometry_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="最近验光单号（只读指针）")
    baseline: Optional[BaselineOut] = Field(None, description="当前生效基线")
    surgery_histories: List[SurgeryHistoryOut] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                                       description="历史手术史")
    contraindication_codes: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, description="禁忌编码")
    updated_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="最近更新时间")


class MergeLogOut(BaseModel):
    """合并与纠错记录出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    merge_no: str = Field(..., description="记录号")
    merge_type: MergeType = Field(..., description="操作类型")
    source_record_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="源档案号")
    target_record_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="目标档案号")
    moved_baseline_count: int = Field(0, description="迁移基线数")
    moved_surgery_history_count: int = Field(0, description="迁移手术史数")
    conflict_count: int = Field(0, description="冲突数")
    exec_status: str = Field(..., description="执行状态")
    apply_by_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="申请人")
    audit_by_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="审核人")
    apply_at: datetime = Field(..., description="申请时间")
    exec_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="执行时间")


class ExportTaskOut(BaseModel):
    """打印导出任务出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    task_no: str = Field(..., description="任务号")
    task_type: str = Field(..., description="任务类型")
    task_status: str = Field(..., description="任务状态")
    progress_pct: int = Field(0, description="进度百分比")
    record_count: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="涉及档案数")
    file_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="文件名")
    file_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="文件地址（带时效签名）")
    file_checksum: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="SHA-256 校验值")
    expire_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="文件过期时间")
    error_message: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="失败原因")


# 解决前向引用
RecordDetailOut.model_rebuild()
```

**Schema 与表的对应关系：**

| Schema | 对应表 | 用途 |
|---|---|---|
| `RecordCreateIn` / `RecordUpdateIn` | `m11_refractive_record` | 建档 / 维护 |
| `BaselineCreateIn` / `BaselineConfirmIn` / `BaselineOut` | `m11_refractive_baseline` | 基线录入与确认 |
| `BaselineEyeIn` / `BaselineEyeOut` | `m11_refractive_baseline_eye` | 眼别明细 |
| `SurgeryHistoryIn` / `SurgeryHistoryOut` | `m11_refractive_surgery_history` | 手术史登记 |
| `MedicalHistoryIn` / `MedicalHistoryOut` | `m11_refractive_medical_history` | 既往史版本化采集 |
| `TimelineEventOut` | `m11_refractive_timeline_event` | 时间轴展示 |
| `MergeApplyIn` / `MergeAuditIn` / `CorrectFieldIn` / `MergeLogOut` | `m11_refractive_merge_log` | 合并与纠错 |
| `ExportRequestIn` / `ExportTaskOut` | `m11_refractive_export_task` | 打印导出 |
| `RefractiveRecordBriefOut` / `RecordDetailOut` / `RecordPageOut` / `RecordContractOut` | `m11_refractive_record`(+从表) | 查询与内部契约 |

---

## 附录 A：表清单速查

| 编号 | 表名 | 中文名 | 主键 | 行粒度 | 预估量级 | 分区 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4.2 | `m11_refractive_record` | 屈光档案主表 | `id` | 一患一档 | 12,000/年 | 无 |
| 4.3 | `m11_refractive_baseline` | 术前基线数据表 | `id` | 每次术前检查 | 20,000/年 | 无 |
| 4.4 | `m11_refractive_baseline_eye` | 术前基线眼别明细表 | `id` | 基线 + 眼别 | 35,000/年 | 无 |
| 4.5 | `m11_refractive_surgery_history` | 历史手术史表 | `id` | 一次手术 | 18,000/年 | 无 |
| 4.6 | `m11_refractive_medical_history` | 既往史与全身情况表 | `id` | 档案 + 版本 | 12,000/年 | 无 |
| 4.7 | `m11_refractive_timeline_event` | 档案时间轴事件表 | `id`+`created_at` | 一次事件 | 80,000/年 | **月度 RANGE** |
| 4.8 | `m11_refractive_merge_log` | 档案合并与纠错记录表 | `id` | 一次治理操作 | 800/年 | 无 |
| 4.9 | `m11_refractive_change_log` | 档案变更审计日志表 | `id`+`created_at` | 一次写操作 | 300,000/年 | **月度 RANGE** |
| 4.10 | `m11_refractive_export_task` | 档案打印与导出任务表 | `id` | 一次打印/导出 | 6,000/年 | 无 |

**表名前缀校验：** 9 张表全部以 `m11_refractive_` 开头，与共享规范第 3.2 节 M11-03 的表前缀 `m11_refractive` 一致。

**数量校验：** 9 张，落在共享规范第 12 章「M11-03 建议 7~9 张」的上限内。

## 附录 B：状态枚举汇总

| 枚举 | 字段 | 取值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 档案状态 | `m11_refractive_record.status` | `ACTIVE` / `CLOSED` / `MERGED` | 在管 / 已结档 / 已合并（与共享规范 13 一字不差） |
| 档案级别 | `m11_refractive_record.record_level` | `STANDARD` / `KEY` / `RESEARCH` | 常规 / 重点关注 / 科研入组 |
| 主导屈光类型 | `m11_refractive_record.refractive_type_dominant` | `MYOPIA` / `HYPEROPIA` / `ASTIGMATISM` / `MIXED` | 近视 / 远视 / 散光 / 混合 |
| 准入结论 | `m11_refractive_record.latest_admission_result` | `PASS` / `HOLD` / `REJECT` / `PENDING` | 可手术 / 暂缓补检 / 禁忌拒做 / 待判定 |
| 基线状态 | `m11_refractive_baseline.baseline_status` | `DRAFT` / `CONFIRMED` / `SUPERSEDED` / `VOID` | 草稿 / 已确认 / 已被替代 / 已作废 |
| 基线来源 | `m11_refractive_baseline.source_type` | `PREOP_EXAM` / `MANUAL` / `EXTERNAL_IMPORT` / `POSTOP_BASELINE` | 术前检查 / 手工 / 外院导入 / 术后重置 |
| 基线用途 | `m11_refractive_baseline.purpose` | `SURGERY_PLANNING` / `RETREATMENT` / `ENHANCEMENT` / `RESEARCH` | 术式方案 / 再手术 / 增效 / 科研 |
| 屈光测量方式 | `m11_refractive_baseline.refraction_method` | `AUTO` / `SUBJECTIVE` / `CYCLOPLEGIC` / `COMBINED` | 电脑 / 主觉 / 散瞳 / 综合 |
| 曲率单位 | `k_unit` | `D` / `MM` | 屈光度 / 毫米 |
| 散光类型 | `astig_type` | `WTR` / `ATR` / `OBLIQUE` | 顺规 / 逆规 / 斜向 |
| 地形图形态 | `topo_pattern` | `NORMAL` / `SUSPECT_KERATOCONUS` / `IRREGULAR` / `ASTIGMATIC` | 正常 / 可疑圆锥角膜 / 不规则 / 散光型 |
| 术式可行性 | `surgery_feasibility` / `eye_feasibility` | `FEASIBLE` / `CAUTION` / `NOT_FEASIBLE` / `NEED_MORE_EXAM` | 可行 / 需谨慎 / 不可行 / 需补检 |
| 手术来源 | `m11_refractive_surgery_history.source_type` | `OUR_HOSPITAL` / `OTHER_HOSPITAL` / `PATIENT_REPORT` | 本院 / 外院 / 患者自述 |
| 日期精度 | `date_precision` | `DAY` / `MONTH` / `YEAR` / `UNKNOWN` | 精确到日 / 月 / 年 / 不详 |
| 屈光术式 | `refractive_type` | `SMILE` / `FS_LASIK` / `TRANSPRK` / `SMART` / `ICL` / `PRL` / `CXL` / `PIOL` / `RK` / `OTHER_REFRACTIVE` | 与 M01-07 3.1.4 一字不差 |
| 手术大类 | `surgery_category` | `CORNEAL_LASER` / `INTRAOCULAR` / `CORNEAL_OTHER` / `COMBINED` | 角膜激光 / 眼内植入 / 其他角膜 / 联合 |
| 制瓣方式 | `flap_type` | `MICROKERATOME` / `FEMTOSECOND` / `SMILE_CAP` / `NONE` | 机械刀 / 飞秒 / SMILE 微透镜 / 无瓣 |
| 瓣蒂位置 | `hinge_position` | `SUPERIOR` / `NASAL` / `TEMPORAL` | 上方 / 鼻侧 / 颞侧 |
| 植入物类型 | `implant_type` | `ICL` / `PRL` / `PIOL` / `NONE` | 眼内植入物 |
| 植入位置 | `implant_position` | `POSTERIOR_CHAMBER` / `ANTERIOR_CHAMBER` / `SUSPENDED` | 后房 / 前房 / 悬吊 |
| 手术结果 | `outcome_result` | `SUCCESS` / `PARTIAL` / `FAILED` / `COMPLICATION` / `UNKNOWN` | 成功 / 部分 / 失败 / 并发症 / 未知 |
| 接触镜类型 | `contact_lens_type` | `SOFT` / `RGP` / `ORTHO_K` / `NONE` | 软镜 / 硬镜 / 角膜塑形镜 / 无 |
| 吸烟情况 | `smoking` | `NEVER` / `OCCASIONAL` / `DAILY` / `QUIT` | 从不 / 偶尔 / 每日 / 已戒 |
| 职业类型 | `occupation_type` | `OFFICE` / `DRIVER` / `OUTDOOR` / `ATHLETE` / `STUDENT` / `OTHER` | 近距离用眼 / 驾驶 / 户外 / 运动员 / 学生 / 其他 |
| 风险等级 | `risk_level` | `LOW` / `MEDIUM` / `HIGH` | 低 / 中 / 高 |
| 时间轴事件类型 | `event_type` | `RECORD_CREATED` / `CONSULT_CREATED` / `PREOP_EXAM_DONE` / `BASELINE_CONFIRMED` / `PLAN_MADE` / `PLAN_AUDITED` / `RESERVATION_CREATED` / `SURGERY_DONE` / `FOLLOWUP_DONE` / `COMPLICATION_REGISTERED` / `RETREATMENT` / `RECORD_MERGED` / `RECORD_CLOSED` / `OPTIMETRY_IMPORTED` | 14 种事件 |
| 事件大类 | `event_category` | `CLINICAL` / `BUSINESS` / `SYSTEM` / `GOVERNANCE` | 临床 / 业务 / 系统 / 治理 |
| 来源模块 | `ref_module` | `M11_01` / `M11_02` / `M11_04` / `M11_05` / `M11_06` / `M11_07` / `M09` / `M10_01` / `M10_09` | 时间轴来源 |
| 合并操作类型 | `merge_type` | `MERGE` / `SPLIT` / `CORRECT` / `REDIRECT` | 合并 / 拆分 / 字段纠错 / 患者号重定向 |
| 执行状态 | `exec_status` | `PENDING` / `EXECUTING` / `DONE` / `FAILED` / `REJECTED` | 待审核 / 执行中 / 已完成 / 失败 / 已驳回 |
| 影响范围 | `impact_scope` | `NONE` / `PLAN_AFFECTED` / `POSTOP_STAT_AFFECTED` | 无 / 影响方案 / 影响术后统计 |
| 日志对象类型 | `object_type` | `RECORD` / `BASELINE` / `BASELINE_EYE` / `SURGERY_HISTORY` / `MEDICAL_HISTORY` / `TIMELINE` / `MERGE` / `EXPORT` | 8 类对象 |
| 日志动作 | `action` | `CREATE` / `UPDATE` / `DELETE` / `CONFIRM` / `SUBMIT` / `AUDIT` / `REJECT` / `MERGE` / `CORRECT` / `CLOSE` / `REOPEN` / `IMPORT` / `EXPORT` / `PRINT` / `SYNC` | 15 种动作 |
| 导出任务类型 | `task_type` | `RECORD_PDF` / `RECORD_PRINT` / `BATCH_EXPORT` / `SURGERY_HISTORY_EXPORT` / `BASELINE_EXPORT` / `QUALITY_EXPORT` | 6 类任务 |
| 导出范围 | `scope_type` | `SINGLE` / `MULTI` / `ALL` | 单档 / 多档 / 全量 |
| 任务状态 | `task_status` | `PENDING` / `RUNNING` / `DONE` / `FAILED` / `EXPIRED` / `CANCELLED` | 排队 / 生成中 / 完成 / 失败 / 已过期 / 已取消 |
| 导出格式 | `export_format` | `PDF` / `XLSX` / `CSV` / `JSON` | 文件格式 |

## 附录 C：关键索引与查询

**查询 1：P-02 契约——按档案号取完整屈光档案（P95 ≤ 100ms）**

```sql
-- Step 1：命中 uk_m11_refractive_record_no
SELECT id, record_no, patient_id, patient_name, status, record_level,
       is_high_myopia, is_prior_refractive_surgery, prior_surgery_count,
       latest_baseline_id, latest_admission_result, last_optometry_no,
       cct_od_min, cct_os_min, axial_length_max, endothelial_min
FROM m11_refractive_record
WHERE record_no = 'RG00001234'
  AND is_deleted = FALSE;

-- Step 2：命中 uk_m11_refractive_baseline_current（当前生效基线，1 条）
SELECT baseline_no, baseline_version, exam_date, surgery_feasibility,
       thin_cornea_flag, dry_eye_flag
FROM m11_refractive_baseline
WHERE record_id = 10086
  AND is_current = TRUE
  AND is_deleted = FALSE;

-- Step 3：命中 idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_baseline（双眼明细，2 条）
SELECT eye_side, sphere, cylinder, axis, cct, cct_thinnest,
       k1_value, k2_value, axial_length, acd, endothelial_density
FROM m11_refractive_baseline_eye
WHERE baseline_id = 20031
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY eye_side;

-- Step 4：命中 idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_record（手术史）
SELECT surgery_seq, surgery_date, refractive_type, eye_side,
       preop_cct, postop_cct, implant_type, outcome_result
FROM m11_refractive_surgery_history
WHERE record_id = 10086
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY surgery_date DESC NULLS LAST;
```

**查询 2：P-03 契约——按患者取在管档案（P95 ≤ 100ms）**

```sql
-- 命中 uk_m11_refractive_record_patient_active（一患一档保证最多 1 条）
SELECT id, record_no, patient_name, status, record_level,
       is_high_myopia, is_prior_refractive_surgery, latest_admission_result
FROM m11_refractive_record
WHERE patient_id = 10021
  AND status = 'ACTIVE'
  AND is_deleted = FALSE;
```

**查询 3：档案列表（P02 页，按状态 + 院区 + 关键字，P95 ≤ 400ms）**

```sql
-- 命中 idx_m11_refractive_record_list；关键字场景命中 idx_m11_refractive_record_name_trgm
SELECT id, record_no, patient_name, gender, age_at_first_visit, status,
       record_level, is_high_myopia, is_prior_refractive_surgery,
       latest_admission_result, latest_baseline_date, created_at
FROM m11_refractive_record
WHERE org_id = 1
  AND status IN ('ACTIVE', 'CLOSED')
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
  AND (patient_name ILIKE '%张%' OR record_no = 'RG00001234')
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 20 OFFSET 0;
```

**查询 4：档案时间轴（P06 页，按档案取事件，仅关键节点）**

```sql
-- 命中 idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_key
SELECT event_seq, event_type, event_label, event_at, eye_side,
       summary, key_metrics, ref_module, ref_no, is_key_node, recorded_offline
FROM m11_refractive_timeline_event
WHERE record_id = 10086
  AND is_key_node = TRUE
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY event_at DESC, event_seq DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

**查询 5：角膜偏薄 + 既往手术的高风险档案清单（P05 / M22 质控）**

```sql
-- 命中 idx_m11_refractive_record_high_myopia 与 idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_thin
SELECT r.record_no, r.patient_name, r.prior_surgery_count,
       r.cct_od_min, r.cct_os_min, r.axial_length_max
FROM m11_refractive_record r
WHERE r.is_prior_refractive_surgery = TRUE
  AND (r.cct_od_min < 480 OR r.cct_os_min < 480)
  AND r.is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY r.cct_od_min ASC NULLS LAST;
```

**查询 6：外院手术待核实清单（P05 工作台）**

```sql
-- 命中 idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_unverified
SELECT record_id, surgery_seq, hospital_name, surgery_date, date_precision,
       refractive_type, recorded_at
FROM m11_refractive_surgery_history
WHERE source_type = 'OTHER_HOSPITAL'
  AND verified_flag = FALSE
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY recorded_at DESC;
```

**查询 7：临床关键字段被修改的审计清单（P08 / M02 审计）**

```sql
-- 命中 idx_m11_refractive_change_log_sensitive
SELECT log_no, record_no, object_type, object_no, action, changed_fields,
       change_summary, reason, operator_name, created_at
FROM m11_refractive_change_log
WHERE record_id = 10086
  AND sensitive_field_hit = TRUE
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY created_at DESC;
```

**查询 8：待审核合并申请（P08 审批工作台）**

```sql
-- 命中 idx_m11_refractive_merge_log_pending
SELECT merge_no, merge_type, source_record_no, target_record_no,
       moved_baseline_count, conflict_count, apply_by_name, apply_reason, apply_at
FROM m11_refractive_merge_log
WHERE org_id = 1
  AND exec_status = 'PENDING'
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY apply_at ASC;
```

**查询 9：待清理的过期导出文件（定时任务，每日 02:00）**

```sql
-- 命中 idx_m11_refractive_export_task_expire
SELECT task_no, file_url, expire_at
FROM m11_refractive_export_task
WHERE task_status = 'DONE'
  AND expire_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
LIMIT 500;
```

**索引设计说明：**

| 索引 | 服务的查询 | 类型选择理由 |
|---|---|---|
| `uk_m11_refractive_record_no` | P-02 主查 | 唯一部分索引，排除逻辑删除与空单号 |
| `uk_m11_refractive_record_patient_active` | P-03 主查 + 一患一档 | **部分唯一索引**，DB 层强制一患一档 |
| `uk_m11_refractive_baseline_current` | P-02/P-03 取当前基线 | 部分唯一，保证同档仅一条生效基线 |
| `uk_m11_refractive_baseline_exam` | M11-02 推送幂等 | 唯一索引兜底重复推送（`IntegrityError` 捕获） |
| `uk_m11_refractive_baseline_eye` | 双眼明细唯一 | 唯一约束保证一眼一条 |
| `idx_m11_refractive_record_name_trgm` | 姓名模糊检索 | GIN + `gin_trgm_ops`，支撑 `ILIKE '%张%'` |
| `idx_m11_refractive_record_tags` | 标签检索 | GIN + `jsonb_path_ops`，支撑标签包含查询 |
| `idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_thin` | 角膜偏薄筛选 | 部分索引，仅覆盖 `cct < 480` 行，体积小命中高 |
| `idx_m11_refractive_timeline_event_key` | P06 默认视图 | 部分索引，仅覆盖关键节点 |
| `uk_m11_refractive_timeline_event_idem` | 事件幂等 | 唯一索引防跨模块事件重复落库 |
| `idx_m11_refractive_change_log_sensitive` | 临床字段审计 | 部分索引，仅覆盖 `sensitive_field_hit = TRUE` |

## 附录 D：配置参数与字典

**系统级参数（部署环境配置，不入业务表）：**

| 参数 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `M11.RECORD.NO_PREFIX` | `RG` | 档案号前缀（固定） |
| `M11.RECORD.NO_SEQ_WIDTH` | 8 | 档案号流水位数 |
| `M11.RECORD.CONTRACT_CACHE_TTL` | 60 | P-02/P-03 契约缓存秒数 |
| `M11.RECORD.DETAIL_CACHE_TTL` | 30 | 档案详情缓存秒数 |
| `M11.RECORD.TIMELINE_CACHE_TTL` | 60 | 时间轴缓存秒数 |
| `M11.RECORD.HIGH_MYOPIA_THRESHOLD` | 6.00 | 高度近视判定的等效球镜阈值（D） |
| `M11.RECORD.THIN_CORNEA_THRESHOLD` | 480 | 角膜偏薄判定阈值（μm） |
| `M11.RECORD.CAUTION_CCT_THRESHOLD` | 500 | 需谨慎判定阈值（μm） |
| `M11.RECORD.AGE_MIN` | 18 | 屈光手术最小年龄（岁） |
| `M11.RECORD.AGE_MAX` | 45 | 屈光手术常规最大年龄（岁，超出需评估老视） |
| `M11.BASELINE.VERSION_MAX` | 20 | 单档基线版本上限（防异常增生） |
| `M11.BASELINE.STALE_DAYS` | 180 | 基线失效天数（超过需重新检查） |
| `M11.BASELINE.CONTRACT_STALE_DAYS` | 365 | 契约返回时标注「基线陈旧」的天数 |
| `M11.LENS.SOFT_STOP_DAYS` | 14 | 软镜术前停戴天数 |
| `M11.LENS.RGP_STOP_DAYS` | 28 | 硬镜术前停戴天数 |
| `M11.LENS.ORTHOK_STOP_DAYS` | 28 | 角膜塑形镜术前停戴天数 |
| `M11.ISOTRETINOIN.STOP_MONTHS` | 6 | 异维 A 酸停药月数 |
| `M11.MERGE.ROLLBACK_HOURS` | 24 | 合并/纠错可回滚时间窗（小时） |
| `M11.EXPORT.EXPIRE_DAYS` | 7 | 导出文件保留天数 |
| `M11.EXPORT.MAX_RECORDS` | 5000 | 单次批量导出最大档案数 |
| `M11.EXPORT.MAX_RETRY` | 5 | 导出任务最大重试次数 |
| `M11.CHANGELOG.RETAIN_MONTHS` | 36 | 审计日志保留月数 |
| `M11.TIMELINE.RETAIN_MONTHS` | 60 | 时间轴分区保留月数 |

**业务字典（枚举取值见附录 B）：**

| 字典类别 | 名称 | 取值来源 |
|---|---|---|
| `REFRACTIVE_TYPE` | 屈光术式 | M01-07 3.1.4 的 10 个术式码，一字不差 |
| `EYE_SIDE` | 眼别 | `OD` / `OS` / `OU` / `NA` |
| `RECORD_STATUS` | 档案状态 | `ACTIVE` / `CLOSED` / `MERGED` |
| `BASELINE_STATUS` | 基线状态 | `DRAFT` / `CONFIRMED` / `SUPERSEDED` / `VOID` |
| `FEASIBILITY` | 术式可行性 | `FEASIBLE` / `CAUTION` / `NOT_FEASIBLE` / `NEED_MORE_EXAM` |
| `TOPO_PATTERN` | 地形图形态 | `NORMAL` / `SUSPECT_KERATOCONUS` / `IRREGULAR` / `ASTIGMATIC` |
| `SURGERY_SOURCE` | 手术来源 | `OUR_HOSPITAL` / `OTHER_HOSPITAL` / `PATIENT_REPORT` |
| `CONTACT_LENS_TYPE` | 接触镜类型 | `SOFT` / `RGP` / `ORTHO_K` / `NONE` |
| `RISK_LEVEL` | 风险等级 | `LOW` / `MEDIUM` / `HIGH` |
| `TIMELINE_EVENT` | 时间轴事件 | 14 种事件（见附录 B） |
| `MERGE_TYPE` | 合并操作类型 | `MERGE` / `SPLIT` / `CORRECT` / `REDIRECT` |
| `EXPORT_FORMAT` | 导出格式 | `PDF` / `XLSX` / `CSV` / `JSON` |

**单位约定（强制）：**

| 量 | 单位 | 示例 | 禁止写法 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 屈光度 | D | `-4.25D`、`+2.50D` | `-4.25`（缺单位）、`-425` |
| 轴位 | ° | `180°`、`90°` | `180`（缺单位） |
| 眼别 | OD/OS/OU | `OD`、`OS`、`OU` | `右眼`、`R`、`L` |
| 角膜厚度 | **μm** | `520μm`、`498μm` | mm 单位数值（如 `0.52`） |
| 眼轴长度 | mm | `26.18mm` | `26.18`（缺单位） |
| 前房深度 | mm | `3.20mm` | — |
| 眼压 | mmHg | `15.50mmHg` | — |
| 角膜曲率 | D 或 mm | `42.50D`、`7.94mm` | — |
| 内皮细胞密度 | cells/mm² | `2650 cells/mm²` | — |
| 切削深度 | μm | `86μm` | `0.086mm` |
| 光学区直径 | mm | `6.50mm` | — |

## 附录 E：建表依赖顺序与 Alembic 迁移

**依赖顺序说明：**

本子模块 9 张表**相互之间没有物理外键**（共享规范 2.6 规定模块内也不建物理外键），因此建表顺序只受**索引表达式依赖**（`pg_trgm` 扩展、分区表）与**数据完整性依赖**约束：

| 序号 | 表名 | 依赖外部表 | 依赖类型 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | — | `mpi_patient`（M03-01） | `patient_id` 应用层校验 |
| 0 | — | `m10_optometry`（M10-01） | `last_optometry_no` / `source_optometry_no` 只读指针 |
| 0 | — | `m10_followup_plan`（M10-09） | 视力档案摘要只读引用 |
| 0 | — | `m11_preop_exam`（M11-02） | `exam_no` 只读引用 |
| 1 | `m11_refractive_record` | 上述外部表（逻辑依赖） | 根实体，**最先建** |
| 2 | `m11_refractive_baseline` | `m11_refractive_record` | 应用层外键 `record_id` |
| 3 | `m11_refractive_baseline_eye` | `m11_refractive_baseline` | 应用层外键 `baseline_id` |
| 4 | `m11_refractive_surgery_history` | `m11_refractive_record` | 应用层外键 `record_id` |
| 5 | `m11_refractive_medical_history` | `m11_refractive_record` | 应用层外键 `record_id` |
| 6 | `m11_refractive_timeline_event` | `m11_refractive_record` | 分区表，独立创建 |
| 7 | `m11_refractive_merge_log` | `m11_refractive_record` | 应用层外键 |
| 8 | `m11_refractive_change_log` | 无（日志可记录任意对象） | 分区表，独立创建 |
| 9 | `m11_refractive_export_task` | `m11_refractive_record` | 应用层外键 |

```python
"""Alembic 迁移：M11-03 屈光档案 9 张表按依赖顺序建表"""

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

revision = "20261101_m11_03"
down_revision = "20261101_m11_02"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None

REQUIRED_UPSTREAM_TABLES = ("mpi_patient", "m10_optometry", "m11_preop_exam")


def _assert_upstream_ready(conn) -> None:
    """建表前确认上游表已存在（M03-01 / M10-01 / M11-02 先于本迁移）。"""
    rows = conn.execute(sa.text(
        "SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public' "
        "AND tablename = ANY(:names)"
    ), {"names": list(REQUIRED_UPSTREAM_TABLES)}).fetchall()
    found = {r[0] for r in rows}
    missing = [t for t in REQUIRED_UPSTREAM_TABLES if t not in found]
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(f"上游表缺失，请先执行上游模块迁移：{missing}")


def upgrade() -> None:
    """建表：9 张表按依赖顺序创建。"""
    conn = op.get_bind()
    conn.execute(sa.text("CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm"))
    _assert_upstream_ready(conn)

                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        comment="历史手术史表",
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m11_refractive_surgery_history_seq",
                    "m11_refractive_surgery_history", ["record_id", "surgery_seq"],
                    unique=True, postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))
    op.create_index("idx_m11_refractive_surgery_history_record",
                    "m11_refractive_surgery_history", ["record_id",
                                                       sa.text("surgery_date DESC")],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))

    # ---------- 5. m11_refractive_medical_history ----------
    op.create_table(
        "m11_refractive_medical_history",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("record_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("history_version", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False, server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("is_current", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("TRUE")),
        sa.Column("collected_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("has_keratoconus", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("on_anticoagulant", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("contact_lens_wear", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("contact_lens_type", sa.String(24)),
        sa.Column("last_lens_off_date", sa.Date()),
        sa.Column("is_pregnant", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("is_lactating", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("contraindication_hit", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("contraindication_codes", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("risk_level", sa.String(16), nullable=False, server_default="LOW"),
        sa.Column("risk_factors", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("systemic_diseases", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("version", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        comment="既往史与全身情况表",
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m11_refractive_medical_history_current",
                    "m11_refractive_medical_history", ["record_id"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE AND is_current = TRUE"))

    # ---------- 6. m11_refractive_timeline_event（分区表） ----------
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m11_refractive_timeline_event (
            id               BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
            record_id        BIGINT NOT NULL,
            record_no        VARCHAR(32),
            patient_id       BIGINT NOT NULL,
            event_seq        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
            event_type       VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            event_category   VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CLINICAL',
            event_label      VARCHAR(128),
            event_at         TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            event_date       DATE,
            ref_module       VARCHAR(16),
            ref_no           VARCHAR(32),
            ref_id           BIGINT,
            consult_no       VARCHAR(32),
            exam_no          VARCHAR(32),
            baseline_id      BIGINT,
            plan_no          VARCHAR(32),
            reservation_no   VARCHAR(32),
            followup_no      VARCHAR(32),
            complication_no  VARCHAR(32),
            surgery_record_no VARCHAR(32),
            optometry_no     VARCHAR(32),
            eye_side         VARCHAR(8),
            refractive_type  VARCHAR(24),
            summary          VARCHAR(512),
            key_metrics      JSONB,
            detail_json      JSONB,
            is_key_node      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            display_order    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            source_event_id  VARCHAR(64),
            idempotency_key  VARCHAR(128),
            recorded_offline BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            org_id           BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
            created_by       BIGINT,
            created_at       TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            is_deleted       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            CONSTRAINT pk_m11_refractive_timeline_event PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
        ) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at)
    """)
    for month_start in ("2026-10-01", "2026-11-01", "2026-12-01"):
        year, month = month_start[:4], int(month_start[5:7])
        next_year, next_month = (year, month + 1) if month < 12 else (str(int(year) + 1), 1)
        part = f"m11_refractive_timeline_event_y{year}m{month:02d}"
        op.execute(
            f"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {part} PARTITION OF "
            f"m11_refractive_timeline_event FOR VALUES FROM ('{month_start}') "
            f"TO ('{next_year}-{next_month:02d}-01')"
        )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS m11_refractive_timeline_event_default "
        "PARTITION OF m11_refractive_timeline_event DEFAULT"
    )

    # ---------- 7. m11_refractive_merge_log ----------
    op.create_table(
        "m11_refractive_merge_log",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("merge_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("merge_type", sa.String(24), nullable=False, server_default="MERGE"),
        sa.Column("org_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False, server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("source_record_id", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("source_record_no", sa.String(32)),
        sa.Column("target_record_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("target_record_no", sa.String(32)),
        sa.Column("target_patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("moved_baseline_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("moved_baseline_eye_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("moved_surgery_history_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("moved_medical_history_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("moved_timeline_event_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("conflict_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("conflict_detail", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("target_table", sa.String(64)),
        sa.Column("target_row_id", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("target_field", sa.String(64)),
        sa.Column("old_value", sa.String(512)),
        sa.Column("new_value", sa.String(512)),
        sa.Column("correct_reason", sa.String(512)),
        sa.Column("impact_scope", sa.String(32)),
        sa.Column("apply_by", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("apply_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("apply_reason", sa.String(512), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("audit_by", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("audit_at", sa.TIMESTAMP()),
        sa.Column("audit_result", sa.String(16)),
        sa.Column("audit_note", sa.String(512)),
        sa.Column("exec_status", sa.String(16), nullable=False, server_default="PENDING"),
        sa.Column("exec_at", sa.TIMESTAMP()),
        sa.Column("exec_error", sa.String(1024)),
        sa.Column("rollback_flag", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("rollback_at", sa.TIMESTAMP()),
        sa.Column("rollback_reason", sa.String(512)),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        comment="档案合并与纠错记录表",
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m11_refractive_merge_log_no", "m11_refractive_merge_log",
                    ["merge_no"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))
    op.create_index("idx_m11_refractive_merge_log_pending", "m11_refractive_merge_log",
                    ["org_id", "exec_status", sa.text("apply_at ASC")],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE "
                                             "AND exec_status = 'PENDING'"))

    # ---------- 8. m11_refractive_change_log（分区表） ----------
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m11_refractive_change_log (
            id                  BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
            log_no              VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            record_id           BIGINT,
            record_no           VARCHAR(32),
            patient_id          BIGINT,
            org_id              BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
            object_type         VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RECORD',
            object_id           BIGINT,
            object_no           VARCHAR(32),
            action              VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            action_label        VARCHAR(64),
            from_status         VARCHAR(24),
            to_status           VARCHAR(24),
            changed_fields      JSONB,
            change_summary      VARCHAR(512),
            field_count         SMALLINT,
            sensitive_field_hit BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            reason              VARCHAR(512),
            operator_id         BIGINT,
            operator_name       VARCHAR(64),
            operator_role       VARCHAR(32),
            client_type         VARCHAR(16),
            ip_address          VARCHAR(64),
            trace_id            VARCHAR(64),
            request_id          VARCHAR(64),
            created_at          TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            is_deleted          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            CONSTRAINT pk_m11_refractive_change_log PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
        ) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at)
    """)
    for month_start in ("2026-10-01", "2026-11-01", "2026-12-01"):
        year, month = month_start[:4], int(month_start[5:7])
        next_year, next_month = (year, month + 1) if month < 12 else (str(int(year) + 1), 1)
        part = f"m11_refractive_change_log_y{year}m{month:02d}"
        op.execute(
            f"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {part} PARTITION OF "
            f"m11_refractive_change_log FOR VALUES FROM ('{month_start}') "
            f"TO ('{next_year}-{next_month:02d}-01')"
        )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS m11_refractive_change_log_default "
        "PARTITION OF m11_refractive_change_log DEFAULT"
    )

    # ---------- 9. m11_refractive_export_task ----------
    op.create_table(
        "m11_refractive_export_task",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("task_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("org_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False, server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("task_type", sa.String(24), nullable=False, server_default="RECORD_PDF"),
        sa.Column("record_id", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("scope_type", sa.String(16), nullable=False, server_default="SINGLE"),
        sa.Column("filter_json", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("export_format", sa.String(16), nullable=False, server_default="PDF"),
        sa.Column("include_images", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("task_status", sa.String(16), nullable=False, server_default="PENDING"),
        sa.Column("progress_pct", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("started_at", sa.TIMESTAMP()),
        sa.Column("finished_at", sa.TIMESTAMP()),
        sa.Column("error_message", sa.String(1024)),
        sa.Column("retry_count", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("file_url", sa.String(1024)),
        sa.Column("file_size_bytes", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("file_checksum", sa.String(64)),
        sa.Column("expire_at", sa.TIMESTAMP()),
        sa.Column("download_count", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("requested_by", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("requested_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("approval_no", sa.String(32)),
        sa.Column("watermark_text", sa.String(128)),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        comment="档案打印与导出任务表",
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m11_refractive_export_task_no", "m11_refractive_export_task",
                    ["task_no"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))

    # ---------- 通用触发器：维护 updated_at ----------
    for table in ("m11_refractive_record", "m11_refractive_baseline",
                  "m11_refractive_baseline_eye", "m11_refractive_surgery_history",
                  "m11_refractive_medical_history", "m11_refractive_merge_log",
                  "m11_refractive_export_task"):
        op.execute(
            f"CREATE TRIGGER trg_{table}_set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON {table} "
            f"FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_set_updated_at()"
        )


def downgrade() -> None:
    """回滚：按反序删除 9 张表。"""
    for table in ("m11_refractive_export_task", "m11_refractive_change_log",
                  "m11_refractive_merge_log", "m11_refractive_timeline_event",
                  "m11_refractive_medical_history", "m11_refractive_surgery_history",
                  "m11_refractive_baseline_eye", "m11_refractive_baseline",
                  "m11_refractive_record"):
        op.drop_table(table)
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_m11_timeline_create_partition(DATE)")
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_m11_changelog_drop_expired(INT)")
```

**迁移注意事项：**

| 事项 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| 上游表检查 | `_assert_upstream_ready` 在建表前确认 M03-01 / M10-01 / M11-02 的表已存在，缺失直接抛错回滚 |
| 分区预建 | 迁移中预建 2026-10 ~ 2026-12 分区，并建 DEFAULT 兜底分区；后续由 APScheduler 每月 25 日续建 |
| `pg_trgm` 扩展 | 姓名模糊检索依赖，需 DBA 权限执行 `CREATE EXTENSION` |
| `updated_at` 触发器 | 复用全模块统一函数 `trg_set_updated_at()`，7 张业务表挂载（两张分区日志表不挂载） |
| 无物理外键 | 全文不建物理外键约束，跨表关系由应用层校验 + 每日对账任务保证 |
| 数据初始化 | 本子模块无内置字典表，枚举以 CHECK 约束固化；参数由配置中心维护（见附录 D） |
| 回滚顺序 | 与建表反序；分区表 `DROP TABLE` 会级联删除所有分区与 DEFAULT 分区 |

## 附录 F：文档维护约定

**本文档的权威性：**

1. 本文档是 M11-03 屈光档案**表结构的唯一权威源**。详细设计文档第 4 章仅为指引块，任何表结构变更必须以本文档为准并同步更新 `M11_屈光手术管理_数据结构设计汇总.md`。
2. 表名、字段名、枚举取值**一经发布不得随意改名**；如需变更须走版本升级（`V1.0 → V1.1`）并在版本历史中登记，同时评估对 `M11_屈光手术管理_03_屈光档案_详细设计.md` 第 8 章规则与第 12 章验收的影响。

**变更流程：**

| 变更类型 | 审批 | 影响评估 |
|---|---|---|
| 新增字段（可空） | 子模块负责人 | 评估索引与查询性能 |
| 新增字段（非空） | 子模块负责人 + 模块负责人 | 必须给默认值或迁移脚本 |
| 修改字段类型 | 模块负责人 + DBA | 必须评估存量数据兼容性 |
| 新增/删除表 | 模块负责人 | 同步更新第 12 章表清单与汇总文档 |
| 修改枚举取值 | 模块负责人 | 同步 CHECK 约束 + 附录 B + 详细设计第 3.3 节 |
| 修改分区策略 | DBA + 模块负责人 | 需制定数据迁移方案 |

**与其他文档的一致性检查清单：**

- [ ] 表数量（9 张）与详细设计第 4 章指引块声明一致
- [ ] 表名与第 12 章表清单一一对应，无遗漏无多余
- [ ] 字段单位与附录 D「单位约定」一致（屈光度 D、轴位 °、厚度 μm、眼轴 mm）
- [ ] 枚举取值与共享规范第 13 节公共枚举一字不差
- [ ] 跨模块引用一律写「只读引用，不建外键」
- [ ] 无物理外键、无物理 `DELETE`、无 MySQL 语法
- [ ] `python` 代码块可 `ast.parse`，`sql` 代码块语法正确

**保留与归档：**

| 数据 | 保留期 | 归档方式 |
|---|---|---|
| 档案主表与从表 | 长期（不删除） | — |
| 时间轴事件 | 60 个月 | 超期分区导出 JSONL 后 DETACH |
| 变更审计日志 | 36 个月 | 超期分区导出 JSONL 后 DETACH |
| 导出文件 | 7 天 | 到期删除对象存储文件，任务记录置 `EXPIRED` |

| 版本 | 日期 | 修订说明 | 修订人 |
|---|---|---|---|
| V1.0 | 2026-10-31 | 首次发布：9 张表（档案主表、术前基线、基线眼别明细、历史手术史、既往史、时间轴事件、合并与纠错、变更审计、打印导出任务）、9 个 SQLAlchemy 模型、25 个 Pydantic Schema、附录 A~F 齐全 | 系统设计组 |

**文档结束**
